/**
 * Data hygiene pass, run once at module load.
 *
 * 1. De-duplicates answers so that an answer can only live in one tier. When
 *    an answer appears in several buckets the *commonest* bucket wins, because
 *    that is the honest description of the answer.
 * 2. Drops aliases whose target no longer exists.
 * 3. Normalises every answer to lowercase so lookups are predictable.
 */
function sanitise(prompt: Prompt): Prompt {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const answers = {} as Prompt['answers'];

  for (const tier of TIER_IDS) {
    const bucket: string[] = [];
    for (const answer of prompt.answers[tier] ?? []) {
      const key = answer.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!key || seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      bucket.push(key);
    }
    answers[tier] = bucket;
  }

  const aliases: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [alias, target] of Object.entries(prompt.aliases ?? {})) {
    const targetKey = target.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (seen.has(targetKey)) {
      aliases[alias.trim().toLowerCase()] = targetKey;
    }
  }

  return { ...prompt, answers, aliases };
}

export const PROMPTS_SANITISED: Prompt[] = PROMPTS.map(sanitise);

/**
 * Lookup by id. Built from the sanitised bank so a restored run always sees
 * the same answers the scorer will grade against.
 */
export const PROMPT_BY_ID = new Map(PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => [prompt.id, prompt]));

/** All answers for a prompt, flattened. */
export function allAnswers(prompt: Prompt): string[] {
  return Object.values(prompt.answers).flat();
}

export const TOTAL_ANSWERS = PROMPTS_SANITISED.reduce(
  (sum, prompt) => sum + allAnswers(prompt).length,
  0,
);

===== src/game/confirmation.ts =====
import type { Prompt } from './types.ts';
import { gradeAnswer, type GradeResult } from './scoring.ts';

export interface ConfirmationMatch {
  /** The prompt the player is currently answering. */
  prompt: Prompt;
  /** What the player actually typed, kept verbatim for the “No” path. */
  typed: string;
  /** The single canonical answer the player will submit if they accept. */
  answer: string;
  /** The grading preview that produced this correction. */
  grade: GradeResult;
}

/**
 * Returns a confirmation only for a *fuzzy* match.
 *
 * Exact, normalised, plural, compact and alias matches are deliberately not
 * confirmations: they are valid answers and must submit immediately. The
 * underlying `gradeAnswer` implementation already rejects ambiguous fuzzy
 * matches, so a returned match is always the single best correction.
 */
export function confirmationFor(
  prompt: Prompt,
  input: string,
): ConfirmationMatch | null {
  const typed = input.trim();
  if (!typed) return null;

  const grade = gradeAnswer(prompt, typed);
  if (!grade.accepted || !grade.corrected || !grade.answer) return null;

  return {
    prompt,
    typed,
    answer: grade.answer,
    grade,
  };
}

===== src/game/engine.ts =====
import type {
  GameMode,
  Prompt,
  PromptResult,
  RunEnding,
  RunState,
  SavedAttempt,
  SavedRun,
  TierId,
} from './types.ts';
import { PROMPT_BY_ID, PROMPTS_SANITISED } from '../data/promptBank.ts';
import { gradeAnswer, hintAnswer, normalise } from './scoring.ts';
import {
  METRES_PER_POINT,
  PROMPTS_PER_DIVE,
  SECONDS_PER_PROMPT,
  STARTING_AIR,
  TIER_ORDER,
  tierIndex,
} from './tiers.ts';
import { dailySeed, mulberry32, randomSeed } from './rng.ts';
import { selectDiveQueue, selectEndlessPrompt } from './selection.ts';
import { zoneForDepth } from './zones.ts';

export const MAX_AIR = 5;

/** Seconds burned per hint. */
export const HINT_SECOND_COST = 8;

export type EngineEvent =
  | { type: 'run-start'; state: RunState }
  | { type: 'prompt-start'; prompt: Prompt; index: number; total: number; remaining: number }
  | { type: 'tick'; remaining: number; fraction: number }
  | { type: 'graded'; result: PromptResult; accepted: boolean }
  | { type: 'hint'; text: string; cappedTier: TierId | null }
  | { type: 'timeout'; prompt: Prompt }
  | { type: 'state'; state: RunState }
  | { type: 'run-end'; state: RunState; ending: RunEnding };

type Listener = (event: EngineEvent) => void;

/**
 * Average tier weight of a prompt's answer list. Higher means the prompt
 * pushes players toward more obscure answers.
 */
function difficultyOf(prompt: Prompt): number {
  let total = 0;
  let count = 0;
  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    const weight = tierIndex(tier) + 1;
    for (const _ of prompt.answers[tier]) {
      total += weight;
      count += 1;
    }
  }
  return count === 0 ? 0 : total / count;
}

const DIFFICULTY = new Map<string, number>(
  PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => [prompt.id, difficultyOf(prompt)]),
);

export class GameEngine {
  private listeners: Listener[] = [];
  private state: RunState;
  private rng: () => number;
  /** Prompt queue for the current run. */
  private queue: Prompt[] = [];
  /** Prompts already used in this run. */
  private usedPromptIds = new Set<string>();
  /** Normalised answers already given in this run. */
  private usedAnswers = new Set<string>();
  private timer: number | null = null;
  private deadline = 0;
  private startedAt = 0;
  /** True while a confirmation is open; the clock is frozen. */
  private paused = false;
  private pauseStartedAt = 0;
  /** Tier ceiling imposed by a spent hint. */
  private tierCap: TierId | null = null;
  /** Whether the current prompt has already been graded (accepted). */
  private locked = false;
  /** Last prompt dealt, used to avoid immediate repeats. */
  private lastPromptId: string | null = null;
  private lastCategory: string | null = null;

  constructor() {
    const seed = randomSeed();
    this.rng = mulberry32(seed);
    this.state = {
      mode: 'dive',
      runId: 0,
      seed,
      index: 0,
      results: [],
      attempts: [],
      score: 0,
      depth: 0,
      air: STARTING_AIR,
      finished: false,
    };
  }

  on(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.push(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((l) => l !== listener);
    };
  }

  private emit(event: EngineEvent): void {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(event);
  }

  get current(): RunState {
    return this.state;
  }

  /** The prompt the player is looking at right now, if any. */
  get currentPrompt(): Prompt | null {
    return this.queue[this.state.index] ?? null;
  }

  /** The full prompt queue, used by storage to restore the exact order. */
  get queueIds(): string[] {
    return this.queue.map((prompt) => prompt.id);
  }

  // -- lifecycle ---------------------------------------------------------

  start(mode: GameMode): void {
    const seed = mode === 'daily' ? dailySeed() : randomSeed();
    this.stopTimer();
    this.paused = false;
    this.rng = mulberry32(seed);
    this.usedPromptIds.clear();
    this.usedAnswers.clear();
    this.tierCap = null;
    this.lastPromptId = null;
    this.lastCategory = null;

    this.state = {
      mode,
      runId: this.state.runId + 1,
      seed,
      index: 0,
      results: [],
      attempts: [],
      score: 0,
      depth: 0,
      air: STARTING_AIR,
      finished: false,
    };

    this.queue = mode === 'endless' ? [this.nextEndlessPrompt()] : this.buildDiveQueue(seed);
    this.state.promptIds = this.queue.map((prompt) => prompt.id);
    this.emit({ type: 'run-start', state: this.state });
    this.beginPrompt();
  }

  /**
   * Rebuilds a saved run so the player can pick up where they left off.
   *
   * The saved run only carries prompt *ids*; the prompts themselves are
   * looked up in the bank again, so a restored run always grades against the
   * current data.
   *
   * Newer saves carry the exact prompt queue for both modes. Older saves have
   * no queue: a dive is rebuilt from its seed, while an endless run is rebuilt
   * from the prompts already played and dealt a fresh one on top.
   */
  restore(snapshot: SavedRun): void {
    this.stopTimer();
    this.paused = false;
    this.rng = mulberry32(snapshot.seed);
    this.usedPromptIds.clear();
    this.usedAnswers.clear();
    this.tierCap = null;
    this.locked = false;
    this.lastPromptId = null;
    this.lastCategory = null;

    const results = snapshot.results
      .map((attempt) => this.resolveAttempt(attempt))
      .filter((result): result is PromptResult => result !== null);
    const attempts = (snapshot.attempts ?? snapshot.results)
      .map((attempt) => this.resolveAttempt(attempt))
      .filter((result): result is PromptResult => result !== null);

    // A run can be saved between answering a prompt and moving on, so the
    // saved index can point at an already-resolved prompt. Resume at the
    // first prompt that has not been resolved yet.
    const resumeIndex = Math.max(snapshot.index, results.length);

    this.state = {
      mode: snapshot.mode,
      runId: this.state.runId + 1,
      seed: snapshot.seed,
      index: resumeIndex,
      results,
      attempts,
      score: snapshot.score,
      depth: snapshot.score * METRES_PER_POINT,
      air: snapshot.air,
      finished: false,
    };

    for (const result of results) {
      this.usedPromptIds.add(result.prompt.id);
      if (result.answer) this.usedAnswers.add(normalise(result.answer));
    }

    const savedQueue = (snapshot.promptIds ?? [])
      .map((id) => PROMPT_BY_ID.get(id))
      .filter((prompt): prompt is Prompt => prompt !== undefined);

    if (savedQueue.length > 0) {
      // New saves carry the exact queue, so the next prompt is always the one
      // the player was looking at when they closed the tab.
      this.queue = savedQueue;
    } else if (snapshot.mode === 'endless') {
      // Older saves had no queue. Rebuild from the prompts already played and
      // deal a fresh one on top.
      this.queue = results.map((result) => result.prompt).slice(0, resumeIndex);
      while (this.queue.length < resumeIndex) {
        this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
      }
      this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
    } else {
      this.queue = this.buildDiveQueue(snapshot.seed);
    }

    const lastKnown = this.queue[this.queue.length - 1];
    this.lastPromptId = lastKnown?.id ?? null;
    this.lastCategory = lastKnown?.category ?? null;

    if (snapshot.mode === 'endless') {
      while (this.queue.length <= this.state.index) {
        this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
      }
      if (this.queue.length === this.state.index) {
        this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
      }
    } else {
      // Fill any missing tail with fresh balanced picks. Older saves may have
      // fewer than seven ids.
      while (this.queue.length < PROMPTS_PER_DIVE) {
        const known = new Set(this.queue.map((prompt) => prompt.id));
        const extra = selectDiveQueue(
          PROMPTS_SANITISED.filter((prompt) => !known.has(prompt.id)),
          snapshot.seed + this.queue.length,
          PROMPTS_PER_DIVE - this.queue.length,
        );
        if (extra.length === 0) break;
        this.queue.push(...extra);
      }
      // Safety net: never point past the end of the queue.
      if (this.state.index >= this.queue.length) {
        this.state.index = Math.max(0, this.queue.length - 1);
      }
    }

    for (const prompt of this.queue) this.usedPromptIds.add(prompt.id);
    const current = this.queue[this.state.index] ?? this.queue[this.queue.length - 1];
    this.lastPromptId = current?.id ?? null;
    this.lastCategory = current?.category ?? null;
    this.state.promptIds = this.queue.map((prompt) => prompt.id);

    // A dive can be saved after its final answer but before the player hits
    // Continue. There is no next prompt to restore, so finish the run cleanly
    // instead of re-asking an already-resolved prompt.
    if (snapshot.mode !== 'endless' && results.length >= PROMPTS_PER_DIVE) {
      this.state.index = PROMPTS_PER_DIVE;
      this.state.finished = true;
      this.state.ending = { kind: 'complete' };
      this.emit({ type: 'run-start', state: this.state });
      this.emit({
        type: 'run-end',
        state: this.state,
        ending: { kind: 'complete' },
      });
      return;
    }

    this.emit({ type: 'run-start', state: this.state });
    this.beginPrompt();
  }

  /** Rebuilds a full result from a saved one, looking the prompt up by id. */
  private resolveAttempt(attempt: SavedAttempt): PromptResult | null {
    const prompt = PROMPT_BY_ID.get(attempt.promptId);
    if (!prompt) return null;
    return {
      prompt,
      raw: attempt.raw ?? '',
      answer: attempt.answer ?? null,
      tier: attempt.tier ?? null,
      points: attempt.points ?? 0,
      outcome: attempt.outcome ?? (attempt.tier ? 'accepted' : 'timeout'),
      secondsUsed: attempt.secondsUsed ?? 0,
      hinted: Boolean(attempt.hinted),
      repeat: Boolean(attempt.repeat),
    };
  }

  private buildDiveQueue(seed: number): Prompt[] {
    return selectDiveQueue(PROMPTS_SANITISED, seed, PROMPTS_PER_DIVE);
  }

  /**
   * Chooses the next endless prompt. Deeper water means harder prompts: we
   * require a minimum difficulty that grows with depth.
   */
  private nextEndlessPrompt(): Prompt {
    const depth = this.state.depth;
    const minDifficulty = 1.6 + Math.min(2.6, depth / 9000);
    const chosen = selectEndlessPrompt({
      prompts: PROMPTS_SANITISED,
      rng: this.rng,
      usedPromptIds: this.usedPromptIds,
      lastPromptId: this.lastPromptId,
      lastCategory: this.lastCategory,
      minDifficulty,
      difficultyOf: (prompt) => DIFFICULTY.get(prompt.id) ?? 0,
    });
    this.usedPromptIds.add(chosen.id);
    this.lastPromptId = chosen.id;
    this.lastCategory = chosen.category;
    return chosen;
  }

  private beginPrompt(): void {
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt) return;
    this.locked = false;
    this.tierCap = null;
    this.paused = false;
    this.startedAt = performance.now();
    this.deadline = this.startedAt + SECONDS_PER_PROMPT * 1000;
    this.emit({
      type: 'prompt-start',
      prompt,
      index: this.state.index,
      total: this.state.mode === 'endless' ? Infinity : PROMPTS_PER_DIVE,
      remaining: SECONDS_PER_PROMPT,
    });
    this.startTimer();
  }

  private startTimer(): void {
    this.stopTimer();
    this.timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const remaining = Math.max(0, (this.deadline - performance.now()) / 1000);
      this.emit({ type: 'tick', remaining, fraction: remaining / SECONDS_PER_PROMPT });
      if (remaining <= 0) {
        this.stopTimer();
        this.handleTimeout();
      }
    }, 100);
  }

  private stopTimer(): void {
    if (this.timer !== null) {
      window.clearInterval(this.timer);
      this.timer = null;
    }
  }

  /** Seconds already spent on the current prompt. */
  get secondsUsed(): number {
    const now = this.paused ? this.pauseStartedAt : performance.now();
    return (now - this.startedAt) / 1000;
  }

  get remainingSeconds(): number {
    const now = this.paused ? this.pauseStartedAt : performance.now();
    return Math.max(0, (this.deadline - now) / 1000);
  }

  /**
   * Freezes the prompt clock, e.g. while a typo confirmation is open. Time
   * spent deciding whether to accept a correction should not cost the player.
   */
  pauseTimer(): void {
    if (this.paused || this.locked || this.state.finished) return;
    this.paused = true;
    this.pauseStartedAt = performance.now();
    this.stopTimer();
  }

  /** Resumes a clock frozen by `pauseTimer`, without charging for the pause. */
  resumeTimer(): void {
    if (!this.paused) return;
    this.settlePause();
    if (this.locked || this.state.finished) return;
    this.startTimer();
  }

  /**
   * Folds paused wall-clock time out of the active timer. Called by resume and
   * by submit so a caller cannot accidentally charge the player for a pause.
   */
  private settlePause(): void {
    if (!this.paused) return;
    const pausedFor = performance.now() - this.pauseStartedAt;
    this.deadline += pausedFor;
    this.startedAt += pausedFor;
    this.paused = false;
  }

  // -- actions -----------------------------------------------------------

  /**
   * Submits an answer. A refused answer costs only the seconds it took and
   * leaves the prompt open; the player can try again with what's left.
   */
  submit(raw: string): PromptResult | null {
    if (this.state.finished || this.locked) return null;
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt) return null;

    this.settlePause();
    const trimmed = raw.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return null;

    const graded = gradeAnswer(prompt, trimmed, this.usedAnswers);
    const secondsUsed = Math.min(SECONDS_PER_PROMPT, this.secondsUsed);

    if (!graded.accepted) {
      const refused: PromptResult = {
        prompt,
        raw: trimmed,
        answer: null,
        tier: null,
        points: 0,
        outcome: 'refused',
        secondsUsed,
        hinted: this.tierCap !== null,
        repeat: false,
      };
      // Refusals are logged, but the prompt stays open: the player can try
      // again with whatever is left of the clock.
      this.state.attempts.push(refused);
      this.emit({ type: 'graded', result: refused, accepted: false });
      // Save straight away so the refusal survives a refresh.
      this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
      return refused;
    }

    let tier = graded.tier!;
    if (this.tierCap !== null) {
      const capIndex = tierIndex(this.tierCap);
      const actualIndex = tierIndex(tier);
      if (actualIndex > capIndex) tier = this.tierCap;
    }

    const points = { plankton: 4, clever: 8, schooler: 16, rare: 32, deepcut: 64, fintastic: 100 }[tier];

    const result: PromptResult = {
      prompt,
      raw: trimmed,
      answer: graded.answer,
      tier,
      points,
      outcome: 'accepted',
      secondsUsed,
      hinted: this.tierCap !== null,
      repeat: graded.repeat,
    };

    this.usedAnswers.add(normalise(graded.answer!));
    this.state.attempts.push(result);
    this.state.results.push(result);
    this.state.score += points;
    this.state.depth = this.state.score * METRES_PER_POINT;

    if (this.state.mode === 'endless') {
      if (tier === 'fintastic') {
        this.state.air = Math.min(MAX_AIR, this.state.air + 1);
      }
    }

    this.locked = true;
    this.stopTimer();
    this.emit({ type: 'graded', result, accepted: true });
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
    return result;
  }

  /** Spends time (and a tier) to reveal a nudge. */
  useHint(): { text: string; cappedTier: TierId | null } | null {
    if (this.state.finished || this.locked || this.paused) return null;
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt) return null;
    if (this.tierCap !== null) return null; // one hint per prompt

    const remaining = this.remainingSeconds;
    if (remaining <= HINT_SECOND_COST) return null;

    const hint = hintAnswer(prompt);
    this.tierCap = hint.cappedTier;
    this.deadline -= HINT_SECOND_COST * 1000;

    this.emit({ type: 'hint', text: hint.text, cappedTier: hint.cappedTier });
    return hint;
  }

  private handleTimeout(): void {
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt || this.locked) return;

    const result: PromptResult = {
      prompt,
      raw: '',
      answer: null,
      tier: null,
      points: 0,
      outcome: 'timeout',
      secondsUsed: SECONDS_PER_PROMPT,
      hinted: this.tierCap !== null,
      repeat: false,
    };
    this.state.attempts.push(result);
    this.state.results.push(result);
    this.locked = true;
    this.emit({ type: 'timeout', prompt });
    this.emit({ type: 'graded', result, accepted: false });

    if (this.state.mode === 'endless') {
      this.state.air -= 1;
      if (this.state.air <= 0) {
        this.finish({ kind: 'out-of-air', fatalPrompt: prompt });
        return;
      }
    }

    // The prompt is over, but the player still gets to see the card and
    // decide when to carry on.
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
  }

  /** Moves to the next prompt. */
  advance(): void {
    if (this.state.finished) return;

    if (this.state.mode === 'endless') {
      this.state.index += 1;
      this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
      this.state.promptIds = this.queue.map((prompt) => prompt.id);
      this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
      this.beginPrompt();
      return;
    }

    this.state.index += 1;
    if (this.state.index >= PROMPTS_PER_DIVE) {
      this.finish({ kind: 'complete' });
      return;
    }
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
    this.beginPrompt();
  }

  /** Ends the run early. */
  abort(): void {
    if (this.state.finished) return;
    this.stopTimer();
    this.finish({ kind: 'aborted' });
  }

  private finish(ending: RunEnding): void {
    this.stopTimer();
    this.paused = false;
    this.state.finished = true;
    this.state.ending = ending;
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
    this.emit({ type: 'run-end', state: this.state, ending });
  }

  // -- reporting ---------------------------------------------------------

  get zone() {
    return zoneForDepth(this.state.depth);
  }

  get tierCounts(): Record<TierId, number> {
    const counts = {
      plankton: 0,
      clever: 0,
      schooler: 0,
      rare: 0,
      deepcut: 0,
      fintastic: 0,
    } as Record<TierId, number>;
    for (const result of this.state.results) {
      if (result.tier) counts[result.tier] += 1;
    }
    return counts;
  }

  get acceptedCount(): number {
    return this.state.results.filter((r) => r.tier !== null).length;
  }

  get refusedCount(): number {
    return this.state.attempts.filter((r) => r.outcome === 'refused').length;
  }
}

export { PROMPTS_SANITISED };

===== src/game/rng.ts =====
/**
 * Small deterministic PRNG so that a "daily" dive is the same for everyone,
 * and so a run can be replayed exactly.
 */
export function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a |= 0;
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

export function randomSeed(): number {
  return Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

/** Seed for today's daily dive — same for every player on the same date. */
export function dailySeed(date = new Date()): number {
  const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth() + 1}-${date.getDate()}`;
  let hash = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < key.length; i++) {
    hash ^= key.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
  }
  return hash >>> 0;
}

/** Fisher–Yates using a seeded generator. */
export function shuffle<T>(items: readonly T[], rng: () => number): T[] {
  const out = items.slice();
  for (let i = out.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(rng() * (i + 1));
    const a = out[i]!;
    const b = out[j]!;
    out[i] = b;
    out[j] = a;
  }
  return out;
}

export function pick<T>(items: readonly T[], rng: () => number): T {
  return items[Math.floor(rng() * items.length)]!;
}

===== src/game/scoring.ts =====
import type { Prompt, TierId } from './types.ts';
import { TIERS, TIER_ORDER } from './tiers.ts';

/**
 * Normalises free text so that "The  Beatles", "beatles!" and "BEATLES" all
 * resolve to the same answer.
 */
export function normalise(input: string): string {
  return input
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') // strip accents
    .replace(/['\u2019]/g, '') // drop apostrophes: "birds' beaks" -> "birds beaks"
    .replace(/#/g, ' sharp ') // keep C# and F# distinct from C and F
    .replace(/\+/g, ' plus ') // C++ and Google+ survive normalisation
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

/** A space-free key, used for "greatwhite" / "Great White" style input. */
export function compact(input: string): string {
  return normalise(input).replace(/ /g, '');
}

/** Irregular plurals worth special-casing. Both directions are listed. */
const IRREGULAR_PLURALS: Record<string, string> = {
  octopus: 'octopi',
  octopi: 'octopus',
  octopuses: 'octopi',
  cactus: 'cacti',
  cacti: 'cactus',
  cactuses: 'cacti',
  fungus: 'fungi',
  fungi: 'fungus',
  mouse: 'mice',
  mice: 'mouse',
  child: 'children',
  children: 'child',
  person: 'people',
  people: 'person',
  goose: 'geese',
  geese: 'goose',
  tooth: 'teeth',
  teeth: 'tooth',
  foot: 'feet',
  feet: 'foot',
  sheep: 'sheep',
  fish: 'fish',
  series: 'series',
  species: 'species',
  aircraft: 'aircraft',
  half: 'halves',
  knife: 'knives',
  leaf: 'leaves',
  loaf: 'loaves',
  shelf: 'shelves',
  wolf: 'wolves',
  dwarf: 'dwarves',
  thief: 'thieves',
  life: 'lives',
  wife: 'wives',
  hero: 'heroes',
  potato: 'potatoes',
  tomato: 'tomatoes',
  piano: 'pianos',
  radio: 'radios',
  photo: 'photos',
  zoo: 'zoos',
};

const LEADING_ARTICLES = new Set(['the', 'a', 'an']);

/** Words that never get pluralised or singularised on their own. */
const STOPWORDS = new Set([
  'the', 'a', 'an', 'of', 'and', 'or', 'in', 'on', 'at', 'to', 'for', 'from',
  'by', 'with', 'as', 'its', 'is', 'it', 'de', 'la', 'le', 'el',
]);

/**
 * Singular/plural variants of a single word.
 *
 * Only ever applied to one-word answers and to the individual words of a
 * longer phrase — never to a whole phrase, because "tiger shark" and
 * "hammerhead shark" must not collapse into each other.
 */
export function wordVariants(word: string): Set<string> {
  const out = new Set<string>([word]);

  const irregular = IRREGULAR_PLURALS[word];
  if (irregular) out.add(irregular);

  // plural -> singular
  if (word.length > 3 && word.endsWith('ies')) {
    out.add(`${word.slice(0, -3)}y`);
  }
  if (word.length > 4 && /(ches|shes|xes|zes|oes|ses)$/.test(word)) {
    out.add(word.slice(0, -2));
  } else if (word.length > 2 && word.endsWith('s') && !word.endsWith('ss') && !word.endsWith('us')) {
    out.add(word.slice(0, -1));
  }

  // singular -> plural
  if (!word.endsWith('s')) {
    out.add(`${word}s`);
    if (word.length > 2 && word.endsWith('y')) {
      out.add(`${word.slice(0, -1)}ies`);
    }
    if (/(ch|sh|x|z|o)$/.test(word)) {
      out.add(`${word}es`);
    }
    if (word.endsWith('f')) {
      out.add(`${word.slice(0, -1)}ves`);
    }
  }

  return out;
}

/**
 * Variants of a multi-word phrase. The whole phrase has to match: a player
 * who answers "shark" has not answered "tiger shark". Only light touches
 * are allowed — dropping a leading article and inflecting the content
 * words.
 */
export function phraseVariants(phrase: string): Set<string> {
  const words = phrase.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return new Set();
  if (words.length === 1) return wordVariants(words[0]!);

  const out = new Set<string>([words.join(' ')]);

  // Leading article: "the beatles" <-> "beatles".
  if (LEADING_ARTICLES.has(words[0]!)) {
    out.add(words.slice(1).join(' '));
  }

  // Inflect each content word in turn: "tiger sharks" <-> "tiger shark".
  words.forEach((word, index) => {
    if (word.length <= 3 || STOPWORDS.has(word)) return;
    for (const variant of wordVariants(word)) {
      if (variant === word) continue;
      const next = [...words];
      next[index] = variant;
      out.add(next.join(' '));
    }
  });

  return out;
}

/**
 * Produces a set of "keys" for a phrase.
 *
 * Single words get singular/plural variants, so "eagles" matches "eagle".
 * Multi-word phrases are matched whole, so "shark" does *not* match
 * "tiger shark" and the player has to earn the points they want.
 */
export function keysFor(phrase: string): Set<string> {
  const base = normalise(phrase);
  if (!base) return new Set();

  const words = base.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return new Set();
  if (words.length === 1) return wordVariants(words[0]!);
  return phraseVariants(base);
}

/** Does the player's answer match an accepted answer? */
function matches(inputKeys: Set<string>, acceptedKeys: Set<string>): boolean {
  for (const k of inputKeys) {
    if (acceptedKeys.has(k)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export interface AnswerCandidate {
  answer: string;
  tier: TierId;
  /** Every exact-match key: canonical spelling, plurals, and aliases. */
  keys: Set<string>;
  /** Space-free variants for missing-space input. */
  compactKeys: Set<string>;
}

const CANDIDATE_CACHE = new WeakMap<Prompt, AnswerCandidate[]>();

/**
 * Pre-computes the exact-match keys for every accepted answer of a prompt,
 * including its aliases. Cached because a prompt is immutable at runtime.
 */
export function candidatesFor(prompt: Prompt): AnswerCandidate[] {
  const cached = CANDIDATE_CACHE.get(prompt);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const aliasKeys = buildAliasKeys(prompt);
  const candidates: AnswerCandidate[] = [];

  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    for (const answer of prompt.answers[tier]) {
      const keys = keysFor(answer);
      const aliases = aliasKeys.get(answer);
      if (aliases) for (const key of aliases) keys.add(key);
      const compactKeys = new Set<string>();
      for (const key of keys) compactKeys.add(key.replace(/ /g, ''));
      candidates.push({ answer, tier, keys, compactKeys });
    }
  }

  CANDIDATE_CACHE.set(prompt, candidates);
  return candidates;
}

/**
 * Pre-computes the key sets for every alias of a prompt, grouped by the
 * canonical answer they point at.
 */
function buildAliasKeys(prompt: Prompt): Map<string, Set<string>> {
  const map = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  for (const [alias, target] of Object.entries(prompt.aliases ?? {})) {
    let keys = map.get(target);
    if (!keys) {
      keys = new Set<string>();
      map.set(target, keys);
    }
    // Aliases are hand-written exact spellings. Expanding them into plural
    // forms creates accidents such as the Greek-letter alias "p" claiming
    // "ps" before the real alias for psi can match it.
    const key = normalise(alias);
    if (key) keys.add(key);
  }
  return map;
}

/**
 * How many edits we are willing to forgive, by the length of the longer
 * string. Short answers stay strict; long answers tolerate a small typo.
 */
export function fuzzyDistanceLimit(length: number): number {
  if (length < 4) return 0;
  if (length <= 12) return 1;
  if (length <= 20) return 2;
  return 3;
}

/**
 * Bounded Levenshtein distance. Returns `max + 1` as soon as it is clear the
 * strings are further apart than we care about.
 */
export function boundedLevenshtein(a: string, b: string, max: number): number {
  if (a === b) return 0;
  if (max <= 0) return max + 1;
  if (Math.abs(a.length - b.length) > max) return max + 1;

  let previous = Array.from({ length: b.length + 1 }, (_, index) => index);
  for (let i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) {
    const current = [i];
    let rowMinimum = i;
    for (let j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) {
      const substitution = previous[j - 1]! + (a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1);
      const value = Math.min(previous[j]! + 1, current[j - 1]! + 1, substitution);
      current[j] = value;
      if (value < rowMinimum) rowMinimum = value;
    }
    if (rowMinimum > max) return max + 1;
    previous = current;
  }

  return previous[b.length]!;
}

interface FuzzyMatch {
  candidate: AnswerCandidate;
  distance: number;
}

function findFuzzyCandidates(inputNorm: string, prompt: Prompt): FuzzyMatch[] {
  if (inputNorm.length < 4) return [];
  const inputCompact = inputNorm.replace(/ /g, '');
  const best: FuzzyMatch[] = [];

  for (const candidate of candidatesFor(prompt)) {
    let distance = Infinity;

    for (const key of candidate.keys) {
      if (key === inputNorm) continue;
      const limit = fuzzyDistanceLimit(Math.max(inputNorm.length, key.length));
      if (limit === 0) continue;
      const next = boundedLevenshtein(inputNorm, key, limit);
      if (next <= limit && next < distance) distance = next;
    }

    // A typo in a missing-space answer ("greatwhit") should still resolve.
    if (inputCompact.length >= 4) {
      for (const key of candidate.compactKeys) {
        if (key === inputCompact) continue;
        const limit = fuzzyDistanceLimit(Math.max(inputCompact.length, key.length));
        if (limit === 0) continue;
        const next = boundedLevenshtein(inputCompact, key, limit);
        if (next <= limit && next < distance) distance = next;
      }
    }

    if (Number.isFinite(distance)) best.push({ candidate, distance });
  }

  return best;
}

export interface GradeResult {
  accepted: boolean;
  tier: TierId | null;
  /** The canonical accepted answer text. */
  answer: string | null;
  /** True when the answer was already used in an earlier prompt this run. */
  repeat: boolean;
  /** True when the answer was resolved through a spelling correction. */
  corrected?: boolean;
}

function accept(candidate: AnswerCandidate, usedAnswers: Set<string>, corrected = false): GradeResult {
  return {
    accepted: true,
    tier: candidate.tier,
    answer: candidate.answer,
    repeat: usedAnswers.has(normalise(candidate.answer)),
    corrected,
  };
}

/**
 * Grades a player's answer against a prompt's accepted list.
 *
 * Matching is intentionally conservative:
 *   1. Exact normalised match (including plural and article variants).
 *   2. Exact alias match.
 *   3. Exact space-free match ("greatwhite" / "great white").
 *   4. A single unambiguous spelling correction.
 *
 * A fuzzy match is only accepted when one candidate is clearly closest. Ties
 * are refused rather than guessed.
 */
export function gradeAnswer(
  prompt: Prompt,
  input: string,
  usedAnswers: Set<string> = new Set(),
): GradeResult {
  const inputNorm = normalise(input);
  if (!inputNorm) {
    return { accepted: false, tier: null, answer: null, repeat: false };
  }

  const inputKeys = keysFor(input);
  if (inputKeys.size === 0) {
    return { accepted: false, tier: null, answer: null, repeat: false };
  }

  const candidates = candidatesFor(prompt);

  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    if (matches(inputKeys, candidate.keys)) return accept(candidate, usedAnswers);
  }

  const inputCompact = inputNorm.replace(/ /g, '');
  if (inputCompact) {
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      if (candidate.compactKeys.has(inputCompact)) return accept(candidate, usedAnswers);
    }
  }

  const fuzzy = findFuzzyCandidates(inputNorm, prompt);
  if (fuzzy.length === 0) {
    return { accepted: false, tier: null, answer: null, repeat: false };
  }

  const shortest = Math.min(...fuzzy.map((match) => match.distance));
  const closest = fuzzy.filter((match) => match.distance === shortest);
  const uniqueAnswers = new Set(closest.map((match) => match.candidate.answer));
  if (uniqueAnswers.size !== 1) {
    return { accepted: false, tier: null, answer: null, repeat: false };
  }

  return accept(closest[0]!.candidate, usedAnswers, true);
}

/**
 * Picks a helpful nudge for a refused answer. We reveal the tier the player's
 * answer *sounds* like it belongs to without giving the answer away.
 */
export function refusalHint(prompt: Prompt): string {
  return `Stay inside the category: ${prompt.hint}. The obvious answer is worth almost nothing here.`;
}

/**
 * The tier a hint reveals an example of — and the ceiling it puts on what
 * the prompt can be worth. A hint is a trade: you buy a sense of direction
 * with the top of the scoreboard.
 */
export const HINT_TIER: TierId = 'rare';

/**
 * Suggests a nearby-but-lower-tier answer for the hint system.
 */
export function hintAnswer(prompt: Prompt): {
  text: string;
  cappedTier: TierId | null;
} {
  const pool = prompt.answers[HINT_TIER] ?? [];
  const example = pool[0];
  if (!example) {
    return { text: 'No examples left to reveal — trust your instinct.', cappedTier: null };
  }
  const meta = TIERS[HINT_TIER];
  return {
    text: `A ${meta.name} answer looks like: “${example}”. Scoring is capped at ${meta.name} for this prompt.`,
    cappedTier: HINT_TIER,
  };
}

===== src/game/selection.ts =====
import type { Prompt } from './types.ts';
import { mulberry32 } from './rng.ts';

/**
 * Prompt selection lives here so it can be tested without a browser, a timer,
 * or an engine instance.
 */

function countCategories(prompts: readonly Prompt[]): Map<string, number> {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const prompt of prompts) {
    counts.set(prompt.category, (counts.get(prompt.category) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  return counts;
}

function leastUsedCategory(
  candidates: readonly Prompt[],
  counts: Map<string, number>,
): string[] {
  const minimum = Math.min(
    ...candidates.map((prompt) => counts.get(prompt.category) ?? 0),
  );
  return candidates
    .filter((prompt) => (counts.get(prompt.category) ?? 0) === minimum)
    .map((prompt) => prompt.id);
}

function choose(
  candidates: readonly Prompt[],
  rng: () => number,
  usedCategories: Map<string, number>,
  lastCategory: string | null,
  lastPromptId: string | null,
): Prompt {
  let pool = [...candidates];
  if (pool.length === 0) throw new Error('No prompt candidates available');

  // Never repeat the immediately previous prompt if there is any alternative.
  if (pool.length > 1 && lastPromptId) {
    const withoutLast = pool.filter((prompt) => prompt.id !== lastPromptId);
    if (withoutLast.length > 0) pool = withoutLast;
  }

  // Prefer a category that has appeared least in this run.
  if (pool.length > 1) {
    const leastUsed = new Set(leastUsedCategory(pool, usedCategories));
    const balanced = pool.filter((prompt) => leastUsed.has(prompt.id));
    if (balanced.length > 0) pool = balanced;
  }

  // Then avoid running two prompts from the same category back to back.
  if (pool.length > 1 && lastCategory) {
    const different = pool.filter((prompt) => prompt.category !== lastCategory);
    if (different.length > 0) pool = different;
  }

  return pool[Math.floor(rng() * pool.length)]!;
}

/**
 * Builds the seven-prompt queue for a dive. The seed makes it reproducible;
 * the category balancing keeps a single run from becoming a one-topic quiz.
 */
export function selectDiveQueue(
  prompts: readonly Prompt[],
  seed: number,
  count: number,
): Prompt[] {
  const rng = mulberry32(seed ^ 0x9e3779b9);
  const usedIds = new Set<string>();
  const usedCategories = new Map<string, number>();
  const chosen: Prompt[] = [];
  let lastCategory: string | null = null;
  let lastPromptId: string | null = null;

  while (chosen.length < count && chosen.length < prompts.length) {
    const candidates = prompts.filter((prompt) => !usedIds.has(prompt.id));
    if (candidates.length === 0) break;
    const next = choose(candidates, rng, usedCategories, lastCategory, lastPromptId);
    chosen.push(next);
    usedIds.add(next.id);
    usedCategories.set(next.category, (usedCategories.get(next.category) ?? 0) + 1);
    lastCategory = next.category;
    lastPromptId = next.id;
  }

  return chosen;
}

export interface EndlessSelectionOptions {
  prompts: readonly Prompt[];
  rng: () => number;
  usedPromptIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  lastPromptId: string | null;
  lastCategory: string | null;
  minDifficulty: number;
  difficultyOf: (prompt: Prompt) => number;
}

/**
 * Chooses the next endless prompt. Prefers fresh, hard prompts; when the bank
 * is exhausted it recycles the hard pool rather than ending the run.
 */
export function selectEndlessPrompt({
  prompts,
  rng,
  usedPromptIds,
  lastPromptId,
  lastCategory,
  minDifficulty,
  difficultyOf,
}: EndlessSelectionOptions): Prompt {
  const hard = prompts.filter((prompt) => difficultyOf(prompt) >= minDifficulty);
  const hardPool =
    hard.length >= Math.min(8, prompts.length)
      ? hard
      : prompts
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => difficultyOf(a) - difficultyOf(b))
          .slice(-Math.min(8, prompts.length));

  const fresh = hardPool.filter((prompt) => !usedPromptIds.has(prompt.id));
  const candidates = fresh.length > 0 ? fresh : hardPool;

  const usedCategories = countCategories(
    prompts.filter((prompt) => usedPromptIds.has(prompt.id)),
  );

  return choose(candidates, rng, usedCategories, lastCategory, lastPromptId);
}

===== src/game/storage.ts =====
import type {
  AttemptOutcome,
  PlayerStats,
  PromptResult,
  RunState,
  SavedAttempt,
  SavedRun,
  TierId,
} from './types';
import { TIER_ORDER } from './tiers.ts';

const STATS_KEY = 'fin-tastic.stats.v1';
const RUN_KEY = 'fin-tastic.run.v1';

export function emptyStats(): PlayerStats {
  return {
    dives: 0,
    endlessRuns: 0,
    totalPrompts: 0,
    bestDepth: 0,
    bestScore: 0,
    bestEndlessDepth: 0,
    tierCounts: {
      plankton: 0,
      clever: 0,
      schooler: 0,
      rare: 0,
      deepcut: 0,
      fintastic: 0,
    },
    usedAnswers: [],
    dailyStreak: 0,
    lastDailyPlayed: null,
  };
}

function safeParse<T>(raw: string | null, fallback: T): T {
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

export function loadStats(): PlayerStats {
  const base = emptyStats();
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return base;
  const stored = safeParse<Partial<PlayerStats>>(localStorage.getItem(STATS_KEY), {});
  return {
    ...base,
    ...stored,
    tierCounts: { ...base.tierCounts, ...(stored.tierCounts ?? {}) },
    usedAnswers: stored.usedAnswers ?? [],
  };
}

export function saveStats(stats: PlayerStats): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STATS_KEY, JSON.stringify(stats));
  } catch {
    /* storage full or blocked — the game still works, it just forgets. */
  }
}

export function recordRun(stats: PlayerStats, run: {
  mode: 'dive' | 'endless';
  depth: number;
  score: number;
  tiers: Partial<Record<TierId, number>>;
  answered: string[];
}): PlayerStats {
  const next: PlayerStats = {
    ...stats,
    tierCounts: { ...stats.tierCounts },
    usedAnswers: [...stats.usedAnswers],
  };

  if (run.mode === 'dive') {
    next.dives += 1;
    next.bestDepth = Math.max(next.bestDepth, run.depth);
    next.bestScore = Math.max(next.bestScore, run.score);
  } else {
    next.endlessRuns += 1;
    next.bestEndlessDepth = Math.max(next.bestEndlessDepth, run.depth);
  }

  next.totalPrompts += run.answered.length;

  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    const count = run.tiers[tier] ?? 0;
    if (count) next.tierCounts[tier] = (next.tierCounts[tier] ?? 0) + count;
  }

  const known = new Set(next.usedAnswers);
  for (const answer of run.answered) known.add(answer);
  next.usedAnswers = [...known].slice(-600);

  return next;
}

export function recordDailyPlay(stats: PlayerStats, today: string): PlayerStats {
  if (stats.lastDailyPlayed === today) return stats;
  const yesterday = new Date();
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
  const yesterdayKey = `${yesterday.getFullYear()}-${yesterday.getMonth() + 1}-${yesterday.getDate()}`;
  const streak = stats.lastDailyPlayed === yesterdayKey ? stats.dailyStreak + 1 : 1;
  return { ...stats, dailyStreak: streak, lastDailyPlayed: today };
}

/**
 * Strips a result down to the bits worth keeping in localStorage. Only the
 * prompt *id* is stored — the prompt itself is looked up in the bank when the
 * run is restored, so a saved run stays tiny even after hundreds of answers.
 */
export function toSavedAttempt(result: PromptResult): SavedAttempt {
  return {
    promptId: result.prompt.id,
    raw: result.raw,
    answer: result.answer,
    tier: result.tier,
    points: result.points,
    outcome: result.outcome,
    secondsUsed: Math.round(result.secondsUsed * 10) / 10,
    hinted: result.hinted,
    repeat: result.repeat,
  };
}

export function saveRun(state: RunState, promptIds: string[] = []): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  const run: SavedRun = {
    mode: state.mode,
    seed: state.seed,
    index: state.index,
    score: state.score,
    air: state.air,
    results: state.results.map(toSavedAttempt),
    attempts: state.attempts.map(toSavedAttempt),
    promptIds,
    savedAt: Date.now(),
  };
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(RUN_KEY, JSON.stringify(run));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

const OUTCOMES: AttemptOutcome[] = ['accepted', 'refused', 'timeout'];

/**
 * Normalises one entry of a saved run. Handles older saves, which embedded
 * the whole prompt object and used a `refused` flag instead of an outcome.
 */
function normaliseAttempt(raw: unknown): SavedAttempt | null {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return null;
  const entry = raw as Record<string, unknown>;

  const embedded = entry.prompt as { id?: unknown } | undefined;
  const promptId =
    typeof entry.promptId === 'string'
      ? entry.promptId
      : typeof embedded?.id === 'string'
        ? embedded.id
        : null;
  if (!promptId) return null;

  const rawText = typeof entry.raw === 'string' ? entry.raw : '';
  const answer = typeof entry.answer === 'string' ? entry.answer : null;
  const tierId =
    typeof entry.tier === 'string' && TIER_ORDER.includes(entry.tier as TierId)
      ? (entry.tier as TierId)
      : null;

  let outcome: AttemptOutcome;
  if (OUTCOMES.includes(entry.outcome as AttemptOutcome)) {
    outcome = entry.outcome as AttemptOutcome;
  } else if (entry.refused === true) {
    outcome = 'refused';
  } else if (tierId) {
    outcome = 'accepted';
  } else if (rawText) {
    outcome = 'refused';
  } else {
    outcome = 'timeout';
  }

  return {
    promptId,
    raw: rawText,
    answer,
    tier: tierId,
    points: typeof entry.points === 'number' ? entry.points : 0,
    outcome,
    secondsUsed: typeof entry.secondsUsed === 'number' ? entry.secondsUsed : 0,
    hinted: entry.hinted === true,
    repeat: entry.repeat === true,
  };
}

export function loadRun(): SavedRun | null {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return null;
  const raw = safeParse<Record<string, unknown> | null>(localStorage.getItem(RUN_KEY), null);
  if (!raw) return null;

  // Runs older than a week are not worth resuming.
  if (typeof raw.savedAt !== 'number' || Date.now() - raw.savedAt > 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
    clearRun();
    return null;
  }

  const results = Array.isArray(raw.results)
    ? raw.results.map(normaliseAttempt).filter((a): a is SavedAttempt => a !== null)
    : [];
  const attempts = Array.isArray(raw.attempts)
    ? raw.attempts.map(normaliseAttempt).filter((a): a is SavedAttempt => a !== null)
    : results;

  return {
    mode: raw.mode === 'endless' || raw.mode === 'daily' ? raw.mode : 'dive',
    seed: typeof raw.seed === 'number' ? raw.seed : 1,
    index: typeof raw.index === 'number' ? raw.index : 0,
    score: typeof raw.score === 'number' ? raw.score : 0,
    air: typeof raw.air === 'number' ? raw.air : 3,
    results,
    attempts,
    promptIds: Array.isArray(raw.promptIds)
      ? raw.promptIds.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string')
      : undefined,
    savedAt: raw.savedAt,
  };
}

export function clearRun(): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  localStorage.removeItem(RUN_KEY);
}

===== src/game/suggestions.ts =====
import type { Prompt } from './types.ts';
import {
  boundedLevenshtein,
  candidatesFor,
  fuzzyDistanceLimit,
  normalise,
} from './scoring.ts';

export interface AnswerSuggestion {
  /** Canonical answer text to insert into the input. */
  answer: string;
  /** Start of the text to highlight inside `answer`. */
  matchStart: number;
  /** Length of the text to highlight. Zero means no direct match. */
  matchLength: number;
  /** True when the suggestion came from a spelling correction. */
  fuzzy: boolean;
}

interface RankedSuggestion extends AnswerSuggestion {
  rank: number;
}

/** A single letter is not meaningful input; two characters is the floor. */
export const MIN_AUTOCOMPLETE_LENGTH = 2;

/** The menu never grows beyond a short, scannable list. */
export const MAX_SUGGESTIONS = 5;

const MAX_FUZZY_RATIO = 0.2;

interface KeyScore {
  rank: number;
  fuzzy: boolean;
}

function scoreKey(query: string, queryCompact: string, key: string): KeyScore | null {
  const keyCompact = key.replace(/ /g, '');

  if (key === query || keyCompact === queryCompact) {
    return { rank: 0, fuzzy: false };
  }

  if (key.startsWith(query)) {
    return { rank: 1 + (key.length - query.length) * 0.01, fuzzy: false };
  }

  if (keyCompact.startsWith(queryCompact)) {
    return { rank: 2 + (keyCompact.length - queryCompact.length) * 0.01, fuzzy: false };
  }

  if (key.split(' ').some((word) => word.startsWith(query))) {
    return { rank: 3 + (key.length - query.length) * 0.01, fuzzy: false };
  }

  // A query inside a word is useful ("una" -> "tuna"), but noisy at two
  // characters, so only allow it once the player has typed something real.
  if (query.length >= 3 && key.includes(query)) {
    return { rank: 4 + (key.length - query.length) * 0.01, fuzzy: false };
  }

  if (query.length >= 4) {
    const limit = fuzzyDistanceLimit(Math.max(query.length, key.length));
    if (limit > 0) {
      const distance = boundedLevenshtein(query, key, limit);
      if (distance <= limit && distance / Math.max(query.length, key.length) <= MAX_FUZZY_RATIO) {
        return { rank: 6 + distance, fuzzy: true };
      }
    }
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * Finds the part of the display text that should be highlighted. Fuzzy
 * matches highlight the whole answer because there is no exact substring to
 * point at.
 */
function highlightFor(answer: string, query: string, fuzzy: boolean): {
  matchStart: number;
  matchLength: number;
} {
  if (fuzzy) return { matchStart: 0, matchLength: 0 };

  const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!needle) return { matchStart: 0, matchLength: 0 };

  const direct = answer.toLowerCase().indexOf(needle);
  if (direct >= 0) return { matchStart: direct, matchLength: needle.length };

  const compactNeedle = normalise(needle).replace(/ /g, '');
  const compactAnswer = normalise(answer).replace(/ /g, '');
  const compactIndex = compactAnswer.indexOf(compactNeedle);
  if (compactIndex >= 0 && compactAnswer === answer.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')) {
    return { matchStart: compactIndex, matchLength: compactNeedle.length };
  }

  const firstWord = normalise(needle).split(' ')[0] ?? '';
  if (firstWord.length >= 2) {
    const wordIndex = answer.toLowerCase().indexOf(firstWord);
    if (wordIndex >= 0) return { matchStart: wordIndex, matchLength: firstWord.length };
  }

  return { matchStart: 0, matchLength: 0 };
}

/**
 * Suggests accepted answers for the current prompt. It deliberately only sees
 * the active prompt's answers, so it can never leak answers from other
 * questions. Rarity and points are never included in the result.
 */
export function suggestAnswers(
  prompt: Prompt,
  query: string,
  limit = 5,
): AnswerSuggestion[] {
  const normalisedQuery = normalise(query);
  const compactQuery = normalisedQuery.replace(/ /g, '');
  if (compactQuery.length < MIN_AUTOCOMPLETE_LENGTH) return [];

  const ranked: RankedSuggestion[] = [];

  for (const candidate of candidatesFor(prompt)) {
    let best: KeyScore | null = null;
    for (const key of candidate.keys) {
      const score = scoreKey(normalisedQuery, compactQuery, key);
      if (score && (!best || score.rank < best.rank)) best = score;
    }
    if (!best) continue;

    const highlight = highlightFor(candidate.answer, query, best.fuzzy);
    ranked.push({
      answer: candidate.answer,
      rank: best.rank,
      fuzzy: best.fuzzy,
      ...highlight,
    });
  }

  ranked.sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank || a.answer.localeCompare(b.answer));

  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: AnswerSuggestion[] = [];
  const cappedLimit = Number.isFinite(limit)
    ? Math.min(MAX_SUGGESTIONS, Math.max(1, Math.floor(limit)))
    : MAX_SUGGESTIONS;
  for (const suggestion of ranked) {
    const key = normalise(suggestion.answer);
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);
    out.push({
      answer: suggestion.answer,
      matchStart: suggestion.matchStart,
      matchLength: suggestion.matchLength,
      fuzzy: suggestion.fuzzy,
    });
    if (out.length >= cappedLimit) break;
  }

  return out;
}

===== src/game/tiers.ts =====
import type { TierId, TierMeta } from './types';

/**
 * The six rarity tiers. Points are what count; depth is points * 10 metres.
 */
export const TIERS: Record<TierId, TierMeta> = {
  plankton: {
    id: 'plankton',
    name: 'Plankton',
    points: 4,
    metres: 40,
    blurb: 'The answer on everyone’s lips. It barely moves you.',
    crowdShare: '~60% of players',
    color: '#686868',
    emblem: '○',
  },
  clever: {
    id: 'clever',
    name: 'Too Clever',
    points: 8,
    metres: 80,
    blurb: 'You thought of the second answer. So did everyone else.',
    crowdShare: '~20% of players',
    color: '#8a8a8a',
    emblem: '◔',
  },
  schooler: {
    id: 'schooler',
    name: 'Schooler',
    points: 16,
    metres: 160,
    blurb: 'A real answer, and not a terrible one.',
    crowdShare: '~10% of players',
    color: '#b0b0b0',
    emblem: '◑',
  },
  rare: {
    id: 'rare',
    name: 'Rare',
    points: 32,
    metres: 320,
    blurb: 'Most people would never have said that.',
    crowdShare: '~5% of players',
    color: '#d9d9d9',
    emblem: '◕',
  },
  deepcut: {
    id: 'deepcut',
    name: 'Deep Cut',
    points: 64,
    metres: 640,
    blurb: 'You went somewhere the crowd never went.',
    crowdShare: '~1% of players',
    color: '#b32733',
    emblem: '●',
  },
  fintastic: {
    id: 'fintastic',
    name: 'Fin-tastic',
    points: 100,
    metres: 1000,
    blurb: 'Nobody else reached this. The whole room is looking at you.',
    crowdShare: '1 in a million',
    color: '#e53945',
    emblem: '✦',
  },
};

export const TIER_ORDER: TierId[] = [
  'plankton',
  'clever',
  'schooler',
  'rare',
  'deepcut',
  'fintastic',
];

export const METRES_PER_POINT = 10;

/** Perfect dive = 7 prompts x 100 points = 700 points = 7000 m. */
export const PROMPTS_PER_DIVE = 7;
export const POINTS_PER_PROMPT = 100;
export const PERFECT_DIVE_POINTS = PROMPTS_PER_DIVE * POINTS_PER_PROMPT;
export const PERFECT_DIVE_DEPTH = PERFECT_DIVE_POINTS * METRES_PER_POINT;

export const SECONDS_PER_PROMPT = 25;

/** Endless mode starts with three tanks of air. */
export const STARTING_AIR = 3;

export function tierIndex(id: TierId): number {
  return TIER_ORDER.indexOf(id);
}

export function pointsToDepth(points: number): number {
  return points * METRES_PER_POINT;
}

export function formatDepth(metres: number): string {
  if (metres >= 1000) {
    return `${(metres / 1000).toFixed(metres >= 10_000 ? 0 : 2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} km`;
  }
  return `${Math.round(metres).toLocaleString('en-US')} m`;
}

export function formatPoints(points: number): string {
  return points.toLocaleString('en-US');
}

===== src/game/types.ts =====
/**
 * Core type definitions for the Fin-tastic game.
 */

/** The six rarity tiers an accepted answer can land in, worst first. */
export type TierId =
  | 'plankton'
  | 'clever'
  | 'schooler'
  | 'rare'
  | 'deepcut'
  | 'fintastic';

export interface TierMeta {
  id: TierId;
  name: string;
  points: number;
  /** Depth in metres gained per answer in this tier. */
  metres: number;
  /** Short flavour text shown on the result card. */
  blurb: string;
  /** Approximate share of the crowd that gives an answer in this tier. */
  crowdShare: string;
  /** Accent colour (CSS colour) used for the tier. */
  color: string;
  /** Emblem glyph. */
  emblem: string;
}

export interface Prompt {
  id: string;
  /** The prompt text shown to the player. */
  text: string;
  /** Broad category, shown as a chip before you use a hint. */
  category: string;
  /** Narrower category revealed by the hint. */
  hint: string;
  /** Accepted answers, bucketed by rarity tier. */
  answers: Record<TierId, string[]>;
  /**
   * Alternative spellings / abbreviations that should resolve to a canonical
   * answer. Keys are matched against the normalised player input.
   */
  aliases?: Record<string, string>;
}

/** How a single attempt at a prompt ended. */
export type AttemptOutcome = 'accepted' | 'refused' | 'timeout';

/** A single answered prompt inside a run. */
export interface PromptResult {
  prompt: Prompt;
  /** Exactly what the player typed. */
  raw: string;
  /** Canonical accepted answer, when the answer was accepted. */
  answer: string | null;
  tier: TierId | null;
  points: number;
  /** How the attempt ended. */
  outcome: AttemptOutcome;
  /** Seconds burned on this prompt, including refusals. */
  secondsUsed: number;
  /** Whether a hint was spent. */
  hinted: boolean;
  /** True when the player had already used this answer earlier in the run. */
  repeat: boolean;
}

export type GameMode = 'dive' | 'endless' | 'daily';

export interface RunState {
  mode: GameMode;
  /** Monotonic identifier for this run (dive number). */
  runId: number;
  /** Seed for the prompt order, so a run can be replayed exactly. */
  seed: number;
  index: number;
  /** One entry per resolved prompt, in prompt order (accepted or timed out). */
  results: PromptResult[];
  /** Every attempt the player made, refused answers included, in order. */
  attempts: PromptResult[];
  score: number;
  /** Depth in metres. */
  depth: number;
  /** Endless mode: remaining tanks of air. */
  air: number;
  /** True once the run is over. */
  finished: boolean;
  /** The full prompt queue, when it is known. Used to restore runs exactly. */
  promptIds?: string[];
  /** Set when the run ends, for the summary screen. */
  ending?: RunEnding;
}

export interface RunEnding {
  kind: 'complete' | 'out-of-air' | 'aborted';
  /** The prompt the player died on, if any. */
  fatalPrompt?: Prompt;
}

export interface PlayerStats {
  dives: number;
  endlessRuns: number;
  totalPrompts: number;
  bestDepth: number;
  bestScore: number;
  bestEndlessDepth: number;
  /** Count of each tier ever landed on. */
  tierCounts: Record<TierId, number>;
  /** Answers the player has already used, so we can flag repeats. */
  usedAnswers: string[];
  /** ISO date strings of days the daily was played. */
  dailyStreak: number;
  lastDailyPlayed: string | null;
}

/**
 * A lightweight record of an attempt. Only the prompt *id* is stored, so a
 * saved run stays small in localStorage even with hundreds of answers.
 */
export interface SavedAttempt {
  promptId: string;
  raw: string;
  answer: string | null;
  tier: TierId | null;
  points: number;
  outcome: AttemptOutcome;
  secondsUsed: number;
  hinted: boolean;
  repeat: boolean;
}

export interface SavedRun {
  mode: GameMode;
  seed: number;
  index: number;
  score: number;
  air: number;
  results: SavedAttempt[];
  /** Every attempt, refused answers included. */
  attempts?: SavedAttempt[];
  /** Prompt order, stored so a restored run keeps the same next question. */
  promptIds?: string[];
  savedAt: number;
}

===== src/game/zones.ts =====
/**
 * The ocean the player sinks through.
 *
 * The first 10,935 m are real. Past Challenger Deep the map is invented and
 * hand-drawn, because a perfect dive only earns you to the trench floor and
 * endless mode deserves somewhere stranger to go.
 */

export interface Zone {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  /** Depth range in metres. */
  from: number;
  to: number;
  /** Short description shown on the depth gauge. */
  blurb: string;
  /** Surface colour of the zone, used for the backdrop gradient. */
  color: string;
  /** Deeper colour of the zone. */
  deep: string;
  /** True for the invented zones below Challenger Deep. */
  invented: boolean;
}

export const ZONES: Zone[] = [
  {
    id: 'sunlight',
    name: 'The Sunlight Zone',

  },
  {
    id: 'abyss',

    color: '#1d1919',

    invented: false,
  },
  {
    id: 'hadal',
    name: 'The Hadal Zone',
    from: 6000,

    color: '#100d0d',
    deep: '#0a0808',
    invented: false,

    id: 'glass-orchard',

    color: '#2a1416',

  },

    id: 'vent-fields',
    name: 'The Vent Fields',
    from: 20000,
    to: 45000,
    blurb: 'Black smokers breathe out metal. Things live here that have never seen the sun.',
    color: '#3a1519',
    deep: '#241014',
    invented: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'drowned-cities',
    name: 'The Drowned Cities',
    from: 45000,
    to: 90000,
    blurb: 'Roads down here. Nobody remembers who laid them.',
    color: '#241014',
    deep: '#180a0c',
    invented: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'long-dark',
    name: 'The Long Dark',
    from: 90000,
    to: 160000,
    blurb: 'The water gives up. The pressure does not.',
    color: '#120709',
    deep: '#0b0506',
    invented: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'shrine',
    name: 'The Shrine',
    from: 160000,
    to: 250000,
    blurb: 'Somebody put something down here on purpose.',
    color: '#0b0506',
    deep: '#070304',
    invented: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'fintastic',
    name: 'Fin-tastic',
    from: 250000,
    to: Infinity,
    blurb: 'Nobody has ever written this down. You are here.',
    color: '#8f1d28',
    deep: '#2a0d10',
    invented: true,
  },
];

export const CHALLENGER_DEEP = 10935;

/** The zone a given depth belongs to. */
export function zoneForDepth(depth: number): Zone {
  for (const zone of ZONES) {
    if (depth < zone.to) return zone;
  }
  return ZONES[ZONES.length - 1]!;
}

/** Progress through the current zone, 0..1. */
export function zoneProgress(depth: number): number {
  const zone = zoneForDepth(depth);
  if (!Number.isFinite(zone.to)) return 1;
  const span = zone.to - zone.from;
  if (span <= 0) return 1;
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (depth - zone.from) / span));
}

/** How far through the whole ocean column the player is, 0..1. */
export function totalProgress(depth: number): number {
  const max = ZONES[ZONES.length - 1]!.from;
  return Math.min(1, depth / max);
}

===== src/main.ts =====
import './styles/main.css';
import { App } from './ui/app';

const root = document.getElementById('screen');
const canvas = document.getElementById('ocean-canvas') as HTMLCanvasElement | null;

if (!root || !canvas) {
  throw new Error('Fin-tastic: missing mount points');
}

const app = new App(root, canvas);

// Exposed so a run can be poked at from the browser console:
//   finTastic.engine.current
declare global {
  interface Window {
    finTastic: App;
  }
}
window.finTastic = app;

  }

    opacity: 1;
    transform: none;
  }
}

  }

    transform: none;
  }

    opacity: 0;
    transform: scale(0.86);
  }
  100% {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: scale(1);
  }
}

  from {
    transform: scaleX(0);
  }
  to {
    transform: scaleX(1);
  }
}

  }

  }

    transform: translateX(0);
