===== src/ui/app.ts =====
import type { GameMode, PlayerStats, RunState } from '../game/types';
import { GameEngine } from '../game/engine';
import {
  clearRun,
  emptyStats,
  loadRun,
  loadStats,
  recordDailyPlay,
  recordRun,
  saveRun,
  saveStats,
} from '../game/storage';
import { OceanBackground } from './background';
import { sfx } from './audio';
import { renderGame } from './game';
import { renderHome } from './home';
import { renderSummary } from './summary';

export class App {
  private root: HTMLElement;
  private engine = new GameEngine();
  private ocean: OceanBackground;
  private stats: PlayerStats = emptyStats();
  private teardown: (() => void) | null = null;
  private savedRun = loadRun();

  constructor(root: HTMLElement, canvas: HTMLCanvasElement) {
    this.root = root;
    this.ocean = new OceanBackground(canvas);
    this.stats = loadStats();

    // Warm up audio on the first interaction anywhere.
    const unlock = () => {
      sfx.unlock();
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', unlock);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', unlock);
    };
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', unlock);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', unlock);

    this.engine.on((event) => {
      if (event.type === 'state') {
        this.persist(event.state);
      }
      if (event.type === 'graded' && event.accepted) {
        this.applyDepth(this.engine.current.depth);
      }
      if (event.type === 'run-start') {
        this.applyDepth(event.state.depth);
        // Persist immediately so starting a new run replaces any older save
        // even if the tab closes before the first answer.
        this.persist(event.state);
      }
      if (event.type === 'run-end') {
        this.showSummary();
      }
    });

    this.ocean.start();
    this.showHome();
  }

  private todayKey(): string {
    const now = new Date();
    return `${now.getFullYear()}-${now.getMonth() + 1}-${now.getDate()}`;
  }

  // -- screens ------------------------------------------------------------

  showHome(): void {
    this.teardown?.();
    this.teardown = null;
    this.applyDepth(0);
    this.ocean.setDepth(0);
    renderHome(this.root, {
      stats: this.stats,
      onStart: (mode) => this.startRun(mode),
      onResume: () => this.resumeRun(),
      hasSavedRun: Boolean(this.savedRun),
      todayKey: this.todayKey(),
    });
  }

  startRun(mode: GameMode): void {
    sfx.unlock();
    // The game screen subscribes to engine events, so it has to exist before
    // the run starts — otherwise the opening prompt is announced to nobody.
    this.enterGame();
    this.engine.start(mode);
  }

  private enterGame(): void {
    this.teardown?.();
    this.teardown = renderGame(this.root, {
      engine: this.engine,
      onFintastic: () => {
        this.ocean.swarm(36);
      },
    });
  }

  private showSummary(): void {
    this.teardown?.();
    this.teardown = null;

    const state = this.engine.current;
    const ending = state.ending?.kind ?? 'complete';

    // Persist the finished run into the lifetime stats.
    const tiers: Record<string, number> = {};
    const answered: string[] = [];
    for (const result of state.results) {
      if (result.tier) tiers[result.tier] = (tiers[result.tier] ?? 0) + 1;
      if (result.answer) answered.push(result.answer);
    }

    const previousBest = this.stats.bestDepth;
    this.stats = recordRun(this.stats, {
      mode: state.mode === 'endless' ? 'endless' : 'dive',
      depth: state.depth,
      score: state.score,
      tiers: tiers as Record<keyof PlayerStats['tierCounts'], number>,
      answered,
    });

    if (state.mode === 'daily') {
      this.stats = recordDailyPlay(this.stats, this.todayKey());
    }

    saveStats(this.stats);
    clearRun();
    this.savedRun = null;

    sfx.surface();

    renderSummary(this.root, {
      state,
      stats: this.stats,
      isBest: state.depth > previousBest && state.depth > 0,
      onAgain: (mode) => this.startRun(mode),
      onHome: () => this.showHome(),
    });

    if (ending === 'out-of-air') sfx.airOut();
  }

  // -- persistence --------------------------------------------------------

  private persist(state: RunState): void {
    if (state.finished) return;
    saveRun(state, this.engine.queueIds);
  }

  private resumeRun(): void {
    const run = this.savedRun;
    if (!run) {
      this.showHome();
      return;
    }
    // Restore the exact saved queue when available; older saves are rebuilt
    // from their seed or already-played prompts.
    this.enterGame();
    this.engine.restore(run);
  }

  // -- depth theming ------------------------------------------------------

  private applyDepth(depth: number): void {
    const factor = Math.min(1, depth / 10935);
    this.ocean.setDepth(factor);
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--depth', String(factor));
  }
}

===== src/ui/audio.ts =====
/**
 * Synthesised sound effects — no audio assets required.
 *
 * Everything is generated with the Web Audio API, so the game ships with a
 * handful of kilobytes instead of a folder of wav files.
 */
export class Sfx {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private master: GainNode | null = null;
  enabled = true;

  private ensure(): boolean {
    if (!this.enabled) return false;
    if (!this.ctx) {
      const Ctor =
        window.AudioContext ??
        (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext })
          .webkitAudioContext;
      if (!Ctor) return false;
      try {
        this.ctx = new Ctor();
      } catch {
        this.enabled = false;
        return false;
      }
      this.master = this.ctx.createGain();
      this.master.gain.value = 0.35;
      this.master.connect(this.ctx.destination);
    }
    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') void this.ctx.resume();
    return true;
  }

  /** Browsers require a user gesture before audio starts. */
  unlock(): void {
    this.ensure();
  }

  private tone(
    freq: number,
    duration: number,
    opts: {
      type?: OscillatorType;
      gain?: number;
      delay?: number;
      slideTo?: number;
      attack?: number;
    } = {},
  ): void {
    if (!this.ensure() || !this.ctx || !this.master) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const start = ctx.currentTime + (opts.delay ?? 0);
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();

    osc.type = opts.type ?? 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
    if (opts.slideTo) {
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20, opts.slideTo), start + duration);
    }

    const peak = opts.gain ?? 0.2;
    const attack = opts.attack ?? 0.01;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(peak, start + attack);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + duration);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(this.master);
    osc.start(start);
    osc.stop(start + duration + 0.05);
  }

  private noise(duration: number, opts: { gain?: number; delay?: number; filter?: number } = {}): void {
    if (!this.ensure() || !this.ctx || !this.master) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const start = ctx.currentTime + (opts.delay ?? 0);
    const frames = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * duration);
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, frames, ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
      data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / frames);
    }
    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buffer;
    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    filter.type = 'lowpass';
    filter.frequency.value = opts.filter ?? 900;
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.value = opts.gain ?? 0.2;
    src.connect(filter);
    filter.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(this.master);
    src.start(start);
  }

  /** Soft bubble for the UI. */
  click(): void {
    this.tone(520, 0.08, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.12, slideTo: 780 });
  }

  /** A low thud as the prompt appears. */
  dive(): void {
    this.tone(180, 0.5, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.18, slideTo: 90 });
    this.noise(0.4, { gain: 0.08, filter: 500 });
  }

  /** Answer accepted — a short ascending blip. */
  accept(): void {
    this.tone(440, 0.12, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.16 });
    this.tone(660, 0.16, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.14, delay: 0.08 });
  }

  /** Answer refused — a muted descending blip. */
  refuse(): void {
    this.tone(300, 0.18, { type: 'sawtooth', gain: 0.1, slideTo: 160 });
  }

  /** Tick as the clock runs down. */
  tick(high: boolean): void {
    this.tone(high ? 1200 : 900, 0.04, { type: 'square', gain: 0.05 });
  }

  /** A Fin-tastic answer — a shimmering chord. */
  fintastic(): void {
    [523, 659, 784, 1047, 1319].forEach((freq, i) => {
      this.tone(freq, 1.4, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.14, delay: i * 0.09 });
    });
    this.noise(1.1, { gain: 0.07, filter: 2400, delay: 0.05 });
  }

  /** A deep cut — a heavy sonar hit. */
  deep(): void {
    this.tone(120, 0.9, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.22, slideTo: 60 });
    this.tone(240, 0.7, { type: 'triangle', gain: 0.1, delay: 0.05 });
  }

  /** Run over. */
  surface(): void {
    this.tone(330, 0.7, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.16, slideTo: 660 });
  }

  /** Air tank lost. */
  airOut(): void {
    this.noise(0.7, { gain: 0.22, filter: 1600 });
    this.tone(200, 0.6, { type: 'sine', gain: 0.14, slideTo: 70 });
  }
}

export const sfx = new Sfx();

===== src/ui/autocomplete.ts =====
import type { Prompt } from '../game/types.ts';
import { suggestAnswers, type AnswerSuggestion } from '../game/suggestions.ts';
import { clear, el } from './dom.ts';

export interface AutocompleteOptions {
  input: HTMLInputElement;
  /** Positioned wrapper around the input; the list is appended here. */
  container: HTMLElement;
  getPrompt: () => Prompt | null;
}

export interface AutocompleteHandle {
  close(): void;
  reset(): void;
  isOpen(): boolean;
  destroy(): void;
}

const SUGGESTION_LIMIT = 5;

/**
 * Accessible combobox/listbox for accepted answers. It is intentionally
 * conservative: no suggestions for empty or very short input, at most five
 * results, and no rarity metadata ever enters the DOM.
 */
export function attachAutocomplete(options: AutocompleteOptions): AutocompleteHandle {
  const { input, container, getPrompt } = options;

  const list = el('div', {
    class: 'suggestion-list',
    id: 'answer-suggestions',
    role: 'listbox',
    'aria-label': 'Suggested answers',
    hidden: true,
  });

  let suggestions: AnswerSuggestion[] = [];
  let activeIndex = -1;
  let open = false;

  container.append(list);

  input.setAttribute('role', 'combobox');
  input.setAttribute('aria-autocomplete', 'list');
  input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  input.setAttribute('aria-controls', 'answer-suggestions');
  input.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'listbox');

  function optionId(index: number): string {
    return `answer-suggestion-${index}`;
  }

  function setActive(index: number): void {
    activeIndex = index;
    const nodes = list.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[role="option"]');
    nodes.forEach((node, i) => {
      const selected = i === index;
      node.classList.toggle('is-active', selected);
      node.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(selected));
    });
    if (index >= 0) {
      input.setAttribute('aria-activedescendant', optionId(index));
      nodes[index]?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
    } else {
      input.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');
    }
  }

  function close(): void {
    open = false;
    activeIndex = -1;
    list.hidden = true;
    container.classList.remove('is-open', 'drop-up');
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    input.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');
  }

  function choose(index: number): void {
    const suggestion = suggestions[index];
    if (!suggestion) return;
    input.value = suggestion.answer;
    close();
    input.focus();
    input.setSelectionRange(input.value.length, input.value.length);
  }

  function openList(): void {
    open = true;
    list.hidden = false;
    container.classList.add('is-open');
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');

    // Flip above the input when there is more room there.
    container.classList.remove('drop-up');
    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
    const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
    const spaceAbove = rect.top;
    if (list.offsetHeight > spaceBelow - 12 && spaceAbove > spaceBelow) {
      container.classList.add('drop-up');
    }
  }

  function render(next: AnswerSuggestion[]): void {
    suggestions = next;
    clear(list);
    activeIndex = -1;
    input.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');

    next.forEach((suggestion, index) => {
      const children: (Node | string)[] = [];
      const start = suggestion.matchStart;
      const length = suggestion.matchLength;
      const end = start + length;

      if (length > 0 && end <= suggestion.answer.length) {
        if (start > 0) children.push(suggestion.answer.slice(0, start));
        children.push(
          el('mark', { class: 'suggestion-match' }, [
            suggestion.answer.slice(start, end),
          ]),
        );
        if (end < suggestion.answer.length) children.push(suggestion.answer.slice(end));
      } else if (suggestion.fuzzy) {
        children.push(
          el('mark', { class: 'suggestion-match suggestion-match-fuzzy' }, [
            suggestion.answer,
          ]),
        );
      } else {
        children.push(suggestion.answer);
      }

      const option = el(
        'div',
        {
          class: 'suggestion',
          id: optionId(index),
          role: 'option',
          'aria-selected': 'false',
        },
        children,
      );

      option.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
        // Keep focus in the input so a click does not blur and close first.
        event.preventDefault();
      });
      option.addEventListener('click', () => choose(index));
      option.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => setActive(index));
      list.append(option);
    });
  }

  function update(): void {
    const prompt = getPrompt();
    const value = input.value;
    if (!prompt) {
      close();
      return;
    }

    const next = suggestAnswers(prompt, value, SUGGESTION_LIMIT);
    if (next.length === 0) {
      close();
      return;
    }

    render(next);
    openList();
  }

  function onInput(): void {
    update();
  }

  function onFocus(): void {
    if (open) return;
    if (input.value.trim()) update();
  }

  function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' || event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      if (!open) {
        update();
        if (!open) return;
      }
      event.preventDefault();
      const count = suggestions.length;
      if (count === 0) return;
      if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        setActive(activeIndex < 0 ? 0 : (activeIndex + 1) % count);
      } else {
        setActive(activeIndex <= 0 ? count - 1 : activeIndex - 1);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (event.key === 'Enter' && open && activeIndex >= 0) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      choose(activeIndex);
      return;
    }

    if (event.key === 'Escape' && open) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      close();
      return;
    }

    if (event.key === 'Tab' && open) close();
  }

  function onPointerDown(event: PointerEvent): void {
    if (!open) return;
    const target = event.target;
    if (target instanceof Node && !container.contains(target)) close();
  }

  input.addEventListener('input', onInput);
  input.addEventListener('focus', onFocus);
  input.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, true);
  window.addEventListener('resize', close);

  return {
    close,
    reset(): void {
      close();
      clear(list);
      suggestions = [];
    },
    isOpen(): boolean {
      return open;
    },
    destroy(): void {
      input.removeEventListener('input', onInput);
      input.removeEventListener('focus', onFocus);
      input.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, true);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', close);
      list.remove();
    },
  };
}

===== src/ui/background.ts =====
import { sfx } from './audio';

interface Bubble {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  r: number;
  speed: number;
  wobble: number;
  phase: number;
  alpha: number;
}

interface Particle {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  r: number;
  vy: number;
  vx: number;
  alpha: number;
}

interface Ping {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  r: number;
  life: number;
  speed: number;
}

interface Krill {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  size: number;
  phase: number;
  hue: number;
}

/**
 * The living ocean behind the game: rising bubbles, drifting marine snow,
 * sonar pings and krill swarms.
 */
export class OceanBackground {
  private canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
  private bubbles: Bubble[] = [];
  private particles: Particle[] = [];
  private pings: Ping[] = [];
  private krill: Krill[] = [];
  private raf = 0;
  private last = 0;
  private running = false;
  /** 0 = surface, 1 = lightless depths. Drives tinting and density. */
  private depthFactor = 0;
  private targetDepthFactor = 0;
  private width = 0;
  private height = 0;
  private dpr = 1;

  constructor(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) throw new Error('2D canvas context unavailable');
    this.ctx = ctx;

    this.resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => this.resize());
  }

  private resize(): void {
    this.dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    this.width = this.canvas.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    this.height = this.canvas.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
    this.canvas.width = Math.floor(this.width * this.dpr);
    this.canvas.height = Math.floor(this.height * this.dpr);
    this.ctx.setTransform(this.dpr, 0, 0, this.dpr, 0, 0);
    this.seed();
  }

  private seed(): void {
    const count = Math.round((this.width * this.height) / 42000);
    this.bubbles = Array.from({ length: Math.min(34, Math.max(10, count)) }, () => ({
      x: Math.random() * this.width,
      y: Math.random() * this.height,
      r: 1 + Math.random() * 3.2,
      speed: 8 + Math.random() * 22,
      wobble: 5 + Math.random() * 13,
      phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
      alpha: 0.05 + Math.random() * 0.15,
    }));
    this.particles = Array.from({ length: 55 }, () => ({
      x: Math.random() * this.width,
      y: Math.random() * this.height,
      r: 0.4 + Math.random() * 1.1,
      vy: 3 + Math.random() * 9,
      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4,
      alpha: 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.11,
    }));
  }

  start(): void {
    if (this.running) return;
    this.running = true;
    this.last = performance.now();
    const loop = (now: number) => {
      if (!this.running) return;
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - this.last) / 1000);
      this.last = now;
      this.update(dt);
      this.draw();
      this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.running = false;
    cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf);
  }

  /** 0..1 — how deep the water is. Deeper means darker and less bubble traffic. */
  setDepth(factor: number): void {
    this.targetDepthFactor = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, factor));
  }

  /** Fires a sonar ping from the centre of the screen. */
  ping(): void {
    this.pings.push({
      x: this.width / 2,
      y: this.height * 0.5,
      r: 10,
      life: 1,
      speed: 520,
    });
    sfx.click();
  }

  /** Releases a swarm of krill across the screen. */
  swarm(count = 60): void {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      this.krill.push({
        x: Math.random() * this.width,
        y: this.height * (0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6),
        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 90,
        vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30,
        size: 2 + Math.random() * 4,
        phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
        hue: 352 + Math.random() * 12,
      });
    }
  }

  private update(dt: number): void {
    this.depthFactor += (this.targetDepthFactor - this.depthFactor) * Math.min(1, dt * 2);

    const lightFade = 1 - this.depthFactor * 0.75;

    for (const bubble of this.bubbles) {
      bubble.y -= bubble.speed * dt;
      bubble.phase += dt * 1.4;
      bubble.x += Math.sin(bubble.phase) * bubble.wobble * dt;
      if (bubble.y + bubble.r < 0) {
        bubble.y = this.height + bubble.r;
        bubble.x = Math.random() * this.width;
      }
    }

    for (const particle of this.particles) {
      particle.y -= particle.vy * dt;
      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;
      if (particle.y < -4) {
        particle.y = this.height + 4;
        particle.x = Math.random() * this.width;
      }
      if (particle.x < -4) particle.x = this.width + 4;
      if (particle.x > this.width + 4) particle.x = -4;
    }

    for (const ping of this.pings) {
      ping.r += ping.speed * dt;
      ping.life -= dt * 0.85;
    }
    this.pings = this.pings.filter((p) => p.life > 0);

    for (const krill of this.krill) {
      krill.x += krill.vx * dt;
      krill.y += krill.vy * dt;
      krill.phase += dt * 8;
      if (krill.x < -30) krill.x = this.width + 30;
      if (krill.x > this.width + 30) krill.x = -30;
      if (krill.y < -30) krill.y = this.height + 30;
      if (krill.y > this.height + 30) krill.y = -30;
    }

    this.ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    this.ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${0.02 * lightFade})`;
  }

  private draw(): void {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.width, this.height);

    // Marine snow
    for (const particle of this.particles) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(particle.x, particle.y, particle.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(196, 190, 186, ${particle.alpha})`;
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Bubbles
    for (const bubble of this.bubbles) {
      const wobble = Math.sin(bubble.phase) * 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(bubble.x + wobble, bubble.y, bubble.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(176, 166, 162, ${bubble.alpha})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(
        bubble.x + wobble - bubble.r * 0.3,
        bubble.y - bubble.r * 0.35,
        bubble.r * 0.32,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2,
      );
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(235, 225, 220, ${bubble.alpha * 1.4})`;
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Krill swarm
    for (const krill of this.krill) {
      const wiggle = Math.sin(krill.phase) * 2.4;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(krill.x, krill.y);
      ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(krill.vy, krill.vx));
      ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(${krill.hue}, 68%, 56%, 0.7)`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, krill.size * 1.9, krill.size * 0.75, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-krill.size * 1.7, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-krill.size * 3, wiggle);
      ctx.lineTo(-krill.size * 1.7, 0);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }

    // Sonar pings
    for (const ping of this.pings) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(ping.x, ping.y, ping.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(229, 57, 69, ${ping.life * 0.26})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
}

===== src/ui/dom.ts =====
type Attrs = Record<string, string | number | boolean | null | undefined>;

/**
 * Tiny hyperscript helper. Keeps the UI code readable without pulling in a
 * framework.
 */
export function el<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(
  tag: K,
  attrs: Attrs = {},
  children: (Node | string | null | undefined)[] = [],
): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(attrs)) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === false) continue;
    if (key === 'class') node.className = String(value);
    else if (key === 'text') node.textContent = String(value);
    else if (key === 'html') node.innerHTML = String(value);
    else if (key === 'style') node.setAttribute('style', String(value));
    else if (key.startsWith('data-') || key.startsWith('aria-')) {
      node.setAttribute(key, String(value));
    } else if (value === true) node.setAttribute(key, '');
    else node.setAttribute(key, String(value));
  }
  for (const child of children) {
    if (child === null || child === undefined) continue;
    node.append(typeof child === 'string' ? document.createTextNode(child) : child);
  }
  return node;
}

export function svgEl<K extends keyof SVGElementTagNameMap>(
  tag: K,
  attrs: Attrs = {},
  children: (Node | string | null | undefined)[] = [],
): SVGElementTagNameMap[K] {
  const node = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', tag);
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(attrs)) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === false) continue;
    node.setAttribute(key, String(value));
  }
  for (const child of children) {
    if (child === null || child === undefined) continue;
    node.append(typeof child === 'string' ? document.createTextNode(child) : child);
  }
  return node;
}

export function clear(node: HTMLElement): void {
  while (node.firstChild) node.removeChild(node.firstChild);
}

/** Formats a number with a thin space as thousands separator. */
export function n(value: number): string {
  return value.toLocaleString('en-US');
}

===== src/ui/game.ts =====
import type { GameMode, Prompt, PromptResult, RunState, TierId } from '../game/types';
import { TIERS, TIER_ORDER, formatDepth, formatPoints } from '../game/tiers';
import { zoneForDepth, ZONES } from '../game/zones';
import type { GameEngine } from '../game/engine';
import { HINT_SECOND_COST } from '../game/engine';
import { refusalHint } from '../game/scoring';
import { confirmationFor, type ConfirmationMatch } from '../game/confirmation';
import { el, svgEl, clear } from './dom';
import { sfx } from './audio';

export interface GameScreenDeps {
  engine: GameEngine;
  /** Fired when the player lands a Fin-tastic answer. */
  onFintastic?: () => void;
}

/**
 * Where the submersible sits on the gauge. The scale is piecewise so the
 * shallow zones stay legible even though they are only a few hundred metres.
 */
function gaugePosition(depth: number): number {
  const stops: [number, number][] = [
    [0, 0],
    [200, 0.06],
    [1000, 0.18],
    [4000, 0.4],
    [6000, 0.6],
    [10935, 0.82],
    [250000, 1],
  ];
  for (let i = 0; i < stops.length - 1; i++) {
    const [from, fromPos] = stops[i]!;
    const [to, toPos] = stops[i + 1]!;
    if (depth <= to) {
      const span = to - from;
      const ratio = span === 0 ? 0 : (depth - from) / span;
      return fromPos + (toPos - fromPos) * ratio;
    }
  }
  return 1;
}

/** Small letterbox panel used by the header. */
function stat(label: string, value: string, accent?: string): HTMLElement {
  return el('div', { class: 'stat' }, [
    el('span', { class: 'stat-label', text: label }),
    el('span', { class: 'stat-value', text: value, style: accent ? `color:${accent}` : '' }),
  ]);
}

export function renderGame(root: HTMLElement, deps: GameScreenDeps): () => void {
  const { engine } = deps;
  clear(root);

  const modeLabels: Record<GameMode, string> = {
    dive: 'Free Dive',
    endless: 'Endless',
    daily: "Today's Dive",
  };

  // ---- header ----------------------------------------------------------
  const modeChip = el('span', { class: 'chip chip-mode', text: '—' });
  const diveCounter = el('span', { class: 'chip chip-ghost', text: '' });
  const dots = el('div', { class: 'prompt-dots' });
  const airRow = el('div', { class: 'air-row' });

  const RING_R = 26;
  const RING_C = 2 * Math.PI * RING_R;
  const ringFg = svgEl('circle', {
    cx: '32',
    cy: '32',
    r: String(RING_R),
    fill: 'none',
    stroke: '#e53945',
    'stroke-width': '5',
    'stroke-linecap': 'round',
    'stroke-dasharray': String(RING_C),
    'stroke-dashoffset': '0',
    transform: 'rotate(-90 32 32)',
  });
  const ringBg = svgEl('circle', {
    cx: '32',
    cy: '32',
    r: String(RING_R),
    fill: 'none',
    stroke: '#2a2a2a',
    'stroke-width': '5',
  });
  const ringSvg = svgEl(
    'svg',
    { viewBox: '0 0 64 64', class: 'timer-ring', 'aria-hidden': 'true' },
    [ringBg, ringFg],
  );
  const timerText = el('span', { class: 'timer-text', text: '25' });

  const exitBtn = el('button', {
    class: 'btn btn-ghost btn-small',
    title: 'Surface (end this run)',
    'aria-label': 'Surface and end this run',
  }, ['Surface']);
  exitBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    sfx.click();
    engine.abort();
  });

  const header = el('header', { class: 'game-header' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'header-left' }, [modeChip, diveCounter]),
    el('div', { class: 'header-center' }, [dots]),
    el('div', { class: 'header-right' }, [
      stat('Score', formatPoints(0)),
      stat('Depth', '0 m'),
      airRow,
      el('div', { class: 'timer' }, [ringSvg, timerText]),
      exitBtn,
    ]),
  ]);
  // Replace the placeholder stats with live ones.
  const scoreStat = header.querySelector('.header-right .stat:nth-child(1) .stat-value')!;
  const depthStat = header.querySelector('.header-right .stat:nth-child(2) .stat-value')!;
  scoreStat.textContent = '0';
  depthStat.textContent = '0 m';

  // ---- depth gauge -----------------------------------------------------
  const gaugeTrack = el('div', { class: 'gauge-track' });
  for (const zone of ZONES) {
    const start = gaugePosition(zone.from);
    const size = gaugePosition(zone.to) - start;
    const seg = el('div', {
      class: 'gauge-zone',
      style: `--zone-start:${start * 100}%;--zone-size:${size * 100}%;background:linear-gradient(180deg, ${zone.color}, ${zone.deep})`,
    });
    gaugeTrack.append(seg);
  }
  const gaugeMarker = el('div', { class: 'gauge-marker' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'gauge-sub' }),
  ]);
  gaugeTrack.append(gaugeMarker);
  const gaugeDepth = el('div', { class: 'gauge-depth', text: '0 m' });
  const gaugeZone = el('div', { class: 'gauge-zone-name', text: 'The Sunlight Zone' });
  const gauge = el('aside', { class: 'depth-gauge' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'gauge-label', text: 'Depth' }),
    gaugeTrack,
    el('div', { class: 'gauge-readout' }, [gaugeDepth, gaugeZone]),
  ]);

  // ---- prompt card -----------------------------------------------------
  const categoryChip = el('span', { class: 'chip chip-category', text: '' });
  const promptText = el('h2', { class: 'prompt-text', text: '' });
  const input = el('input', {
    class: 'answer-input',
    type: 'text',
    autocomplete: 'off',
    autocapitalize: 'off',
    spellcheck: 'false',
    placeholder: 'type an answer…',
    'aria-label': 'Your answer',
    maxlength: '80',
  });
  const submitBtn = el('button', { class: 'btn btn-primary', type: 'submit' }, ['Dive']);
  const hintBtn = el('button', { class: 'btn btn-ghost', type: 'button' }, [
    'Hint',
    el('span', { class: 'hint-cost', text: `−${HINT_SECOND_COST}s` }),
  ]);
  const hintNote = el('p', { class: 'hint-note', text: '' });
  const feedback = el('p', { class: 'feedback', text: '', 'aria-live': 'polite' });
  const answerField = el('div', { class: 'answer-field' }, [input]);
  const form = el('form', { class: 'answer-form', autocomplete: 'off' }, [
    answerField,
    submitBtn,
  ]);
  const promptCard = el('section', { class: 'prompt-card' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'prompt-meta' }, [categoryChip, el('span', { class: 'prompt-index', text: '' })]),
    promptText,
    form,
    el('div', { class: 'prompt-tools' }, [hintBtn, hintNote]),
    feedback,
  ]);

  const stage = el('div', { class: 'game-stage' }, [gauge, promptCard]);

  // ---- result overlay --------------------------------------------------
  const resultCard = el('div', { class: 'result-card', 'aria-live': 'polite' });
  const resultOverlay = el('div', { class: 'result-overlay' }, [resultCard]);
  resultOverlay.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (event.target === resultOverlay) nextPrompt();
  });

  // ---- confirmation overlay -------------------------------------------
  const confirmQuestion = el('p', { class: 'confirm-question', text: '' });
  const confirmTitle = el('h3', {
    class: 'confirm-title',
    id: 'confirm-title',
    text: 'Did you mean',
  });
  const confirmAnswer = el('p', {
    class: 'confirm-answer',
    id: 'confirm-answer',
    text: '',
  });
  const confirmNo = el('button', {
    class: 'btn btn-ghost confirm-no',
    type: 'button',
  }, ['No, go back']);
  const confirmYes = el('button', {
    class: 'btn btn-primary confirm-yes',
    type: 'button',
  }, ['Yes, use this answer']);
  const confirmCard = el('div', {
    class: 'confirm-card',
    role: 'dialog',
    'aria-modal': 'true',
    'aria-labelledby': 'confirm-title',
    'aria-describedby': 'confirm-answer',
  }, [
    el('span', { class: 'confirm-kicker', text: 'Possible typo' }),
    confirmQuestion,
    confirmTitle,
    confirmAnswer,
    el('div', { class: 'confirm-actions' }, [confirmNo, confirmYes]),
  ]);
  const confirmOverlay = el('div', { class: 'confirm-overlay' }, [confirmCard]);

  root.append(header, stage, confirmOverlay, resultOverlay);

  // ---- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------
  let lastTickSecond = -1;
  let pendingConfirmation: ConfirmationMatch | null = null;

  function setScore(points: number): void {
    scoreStat.textContent = formatPoints(points);
  }

  function setDepth(depth: number): void {
    depthStat.textContent = formatDepth(depth);
    gaugeDepth.textContent = formatDepth(depth);
    const zone = zoneForDepth(depth);
    gaugeZone.textContent = zone.name;
    const pos = gaugePosition(depth);
    gaugeMarker.style.setProperty('--gauge-pos', `${pos * 100}%`);
    gaugeMarker.style.setProperty('--zone', zone.color);
  }

  function renderDots(state: RunState): void {
    clear(dots);
    if (state.mode === 'endless') {
      dots.append(
        el('span', {
          class: 'dot dot-active',
          title: `Prompt ${state.index + 1}`,
        }),
      );
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        dots.append(el('span', { class: 'dot' }));
      }
      return;
    }
    const total = 7;
    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      const result = state.results[i];
      const done = Boolean(result);
      const dot = el('span', {
        class: `dot${done ? ' dot-done' : ''}${i === state.index ? ' dot-active' : ''}`,
        style: done && result?.tier ? `--dot:${TIERS[result.tier].color}` : '',
      });
      dots.append(dot);
    }
  }

  function renderAir(air: number): void {
    clear(airRow);
    airRow.append(el('span', { class: 'stat-label', text: 'Air' }));
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(3, air); i++) {
      airRow.append(
        el('span', {
          class: `tank${i < air ? ' tank-full' : ''}`,
          title: i < air ? 'Tank of air' : 'Empty',
        }),
      );
    }
  }

  function setTimer(remaining: number, fraction: number): void {
    const seconds = Math.ceil(remaining);
    timerText.textContent = String(seconds);
    ringFg.setAttribute('stroke-dashoffset', String(RING_C * (1 - fraction)));
    const urgent = remaining <= 5;
    header.classList.toggle('urgent', urgent);
    ringFg.setAttribute('stroke', urgent ? '#ff5a64' : '#e53945');
    if (seconds !== lastTickSecond) {
      lastTickSecond = seconds;
      if (seconds <= 5 && seconds > 0) sfx.tick(seconds <= 3);
    }
  }

  function setBusy(busy: boolean): void {
    input.disabled = busy;
    submitBtn.disabled = busy;
    hintBtn.disabled = busy;
    input.classList.toggle('is-disabled', busy);
  }

  function closeConfirmation(
    options: { resume?: boolean; focus?: boolean } = {},
  ): void {
    if (!pendingConfirmation) return;
    const { resume = true, focus = true } = options;
    pendingConfirmation = null;
    confirmOverlay.classList.remove('is-open');
    if (resume && !engine.current.finished) engine.resumeTimer();
    setBusy(false);
    if (focus) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        input.focus();
        const end = input.value.length;
        input.setSelectionRange(end, end);
      });
    }
  }

  function openConfirmation(match: ConfirmationMatch): void {
    if (pendingConfirmation) return;
    pendingConfirmation = match;
    clearFeedback();
    confirmQuestion.textContent = match.prompt.text;
    confirmAnswer.textContent = `“${match.answer}”?`;
    // Freeze the clock before the player starts deciding. The answer is not
    // submitted until they explicitly choose Yes.
    engine.pauseTimer();
    setBusy(true);
    confirmOverlay.classList.add('is-open');
    // Focus the safe action first so a stray Enter or Space cancels rather
    // than scoring a correction the player has not read.
    requestAnimationFrame(() => confirmNo.focus());
  }

  function acceptConfirmation(): void {
    if (!pendingConfirmation) return;
    const match = pendingConfirmation;
    if (
      engine.current.finished ||
      engine.currentPrompt?.id !== match.prompt.id
    ) {
      closeConfirmation({ resume: false });
      return;
    }

    // Close the dialog before submitting, then hand the canonical answer to
    // the existing engine so repeat detection, hint caps and stats all apply.
    pendingConfirmation = null;
    confirmOverlay.classList.remove('is-open');
    engine.resumeTimer();
    const result = engine.submit(match.answer);
    if (!result) {
      // The prompt was resolved elsewhere while the dialog was open.
      setBusy(false);
      if (!engine.current.finished) requestAnimationFrame(() => input.focus());
    }
  }

  function showHint(text: string): void {
    hintNote.textContent = text;
    hintNote.classList.add('is-visible');
  }

  function showFeedback(text: string, kind: 'bad' | 'neutral'): void {
    feedback.textContent = text;
    feedback.className = `feedback is-visible feedback-${kind}`;
  }

  function clearFeedback(): void {
    feedback.textContent = '';
    feedback.className = 'feedback';
  }

  // ---- result rendering ------------------------------------------------
  function openResult(): void {
    resultOverlay.classList.add('is-open');
    // Move keyboard focus off the now-disabled input and onto the next action.
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      resultCard.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.result-actions .btn-primary')?.focus();
    });
  }

  function renderResult(result: PromptResult, accepted: boolean): void {
    clear(resultCard);

    if (!accepted) {
      const timedOut = result.outcome === 'timeout';
      const typedNote = timedOut
        ? null
        : el('p', { class: 'result-answer', text: `You typed: “${result.raw}”` });
      resultCard.append(
        el('div', { class: 'result-emblem refused', text: timedOut ? '—' : '×' }),
        el('h3', { class: 'result-title refused', text: timedOut ? 'Time up' : 'Refused' }),
        el('p', {
          class: 'result-blurb',
          text: timedOut
            ? 'The clock ran out. Nothing scored, nothing punished.'
            : 'That answer is not on the sheet. Refused, not punished — it only cost you the seconds.',
        }),
        ...(typedNote ? [typedNote] : []),
        el('div', { class: 'result-actions' }, [nextButton()]),
      );
      resultOverlay.dataset.tier = 'refused';
      openResult();
      return;
    }

    const tier = result.tier!;
    const meta = TIERS[tier];
    const gained = result.points * 10;
    setBusy(true);

    resultCard.append(
      el('div', { class: 'result-emblem', text: meta.emblem, style: `color:${meta.color}` }),
      el('h3', { class: 'result-title', text: meta.name, style: `color:${meta.color}` }),
      el('p', { class: 'result-blurb', text: meta.blurb }),
      el('div', { class: 'result-points' }, [
        el('span', { class: 'result-points-value', text: `+${result.points}` }),
        el('span', { class: 'result-points-label', text: `points  ·  ${formatDepth(gained)} deeper` }),
      ]),
      el('p', { class: 'result-answer', text: `“${result.answer}”` }),
      el('p', { class: 'result-crowd', text: meta.crowdShare }),
      ...(result.hinted
        ? [el('p', { class: 'result-hint-note', text: 'Scored with a hint — tier capped.' })]
        : []),
      ...(result.repeat
        ? [el('p', { class: 'result-hint-note', text: 'You have said that one already.' })]
        : []),
      el('div', { class: 'result-actions' }, [nextButton()]),
    );
    resultOverlay.dataset.tier = tier;
    openResult();
  }

  function nextButton(): HTMLElement {
    const btn = el('button', { class: 'btn btn-primary btn-wide', type: 'button' }, [
      'Continue ▸',
    ]);
    btn.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      event.stopPropagation();
      nextPrompt();
    });
    return btn;
  }

  function nextPrompt(): void {
    resultOverlay.classList.remove('is-open');
    engine.advance();
  }

  // ---- event wiring ----------------------------------------------------
  const unsubscribe = engine.on((event) => {
    switch (event.type) {
      case 'run-start': {
        const state = event.state;
        modeChip.textContent = modeLabels[state.mode];
        diveCounter.textContent =
          state.mode === 'daily' ? 'everyone gets this one' : `run #${state.runId}`;
        // A new run starts from zero, even when the screen was reused.
        setScore(state.score);
        setDepth(state.depth);
        renderDots(state);
        clear(airRow);
        if (state.mode === 'endless') renderAir(state.air);
        break;
      }
      case 'prompt-start': {
        const prompt: Prompt = event.prompt;
        categoryChip.textContent = prompt.category;
        promptText.textContent = prompt.text;
        (promptCard.querySelector('.prompt-index') as HTMLElement).textContent =
          event.total === Infinity ? `prompt ${event.index + 1}` : `${event.index + 1} / ${event.total}`;
        input.value = '';
        setBusy(false);
        hintNote.classList.remove('is-visible');
        hintNote.textContent = '';
        clearFeedback();
        autocomplete.reset();
        promptCard.classList.remove('is-refused');
        resultOverlay.classList.remove('is-open');
        lastTickSecond = -1;
        setTimer(event.remaining, 1);
        requestAnimationFrame(() => input.focus());
        break;
      }
      case 'tick': {
        setTimer(event.remaining, event.fraction);
        break;
      }
      case 'hint': {
        showHint(event.text);
        sfx.click();
        break;
      }
      case 'graded': {
        if (event.accepted) {
          sfx.accept();
          const tier = event.result.tier!;
          if (tier === 'fintastic') {
            sfx.fintastic();
            deps.onFintastic?.();
          } else if (tier === 'deepcut') {
            sfx.deep();
          }
          setScore(engine.current.score);
          setDepth(engine.current.depth);
          renderDots(engine.current);
          renderResult(event.result, true);
        } else if (event.result.outcome === 'timeout') {
          // The clock ran out: show the card and let the player move on.
          sfx.refuse();
          autocomplete.close();
          setBusy(true);
          renderResult(event.result, false);
        } else {
          sfx.refuse();
          autocomplete.reset();
          const prompt = event.result.prompt;
          const hint = refusalHint(prompt);
          showFeedback(hint, 'bad');
          promptCard.classList.add('is-refused');
          setTimeout(() => promptCard.classList.remove('is-refused'), 600);
          input.select();
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'timeout': {
        break;
      }
      case 'state': {
        setScore(event.state.score);
        setDepth(event.state.depth);
        renderDots(event.state);
        if (event.state.mode === 'endless') renderAir(event.state.air);
        break;
      }
      case 'run-end': {
        renderDots(event.state);
        break;
      }
    }
  });

  form.addEventListener('submit', (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    autocomplete.close();
    const value = input.value.trim();
    if (!value) {
      showFeedback('Say something first.', 'neutral');
      return;
    }
    const result = engine.submit(value);
    if (result && result.outcome === 'accepted') {
      clearFeedback();
    }
  });

  hintBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const hint = engine.useHint();
    if (!hint) {
      showFeedback('No hint left for this prompt.', 'neutral');
    }
  });

  // Keyboard shortcut: Enter submits (native), Escape closes the suggest
  // list first, then surfaces on the next press.
  const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape' && !autocomplete.isOpen() && !engine.current.finished) {
      engine.abort();
    }
  };
  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);

  // Initial paint
  renderDots(engine.current);
  setScore(engine.current.score);
  setDepth(engine.current.depth);

  return () => {
    unsubscribe();
    autocomplete.destroy();
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  };
}

/** A compact legend of the six tiers, used on the home and summary screens. */
export function tierLegend(): HTMLElement {
  return el(
    'div',
    { class: 'tier-legend' },
    TIER_ORDER.map((id: TierId) => {
      const meta = TIERS[id];
      return el('div', { class: 'tier-legend-item' }, [
        el('span', { class: 'tier-legend-dot', style: `background:${meta.color}` }),
        el('span', { class: 'tier-legend-name', text: meta.name }),
        el('span', { class: 'tier-legend-points', text: `${meta.points}` }),
      ]);
    }),
  );
}

===== src/ui/home.ts =====
import type { GameMode, PlayerStats } from '../game/types';
import { formatDepth, formatPoints, PERFECT_DIVE_DEPTH, PERFECT_DIVE_POINTS } from '../game/tiers';
import { PROMPTS_SANITISED, TOTAL_ANSWERS } from '../data/promptBank';
import { el, clear } from './dom';
import { sfx } from './audio';
import { tierLegend } from './game';

export interface HomeDeps {
  stats: PlayerStats;
  onStart: (mode: GameMode) => void;
  onResume: () => void;
  hasSavedRun: boolean;
  todayKey: string;
}

function modeCard(
  mode: GameMode,
  title: string,
  subtitle: string,
  detail: string,
  onClick: () => void,
  featured = false,
): HTMLElement {
  const btn = el('button', {
    class: `mode-card${featured ? ' mode-card-featured' : ''}`,
    'data-mode': mode,
  }, [
    el('span', { class: 'mode-body' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'mode-title', text: title }),
      el('span', { class: 'mode-subtitle', text: subtitle }),
      el('span', { class: 'mode-detail', text: detail }),
    ]),
    el('span', { class: 'mode-arrow', text: '▸' }),
  ]);
  btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    sfx.unlock();
    sfx.click();
    onClick();
  });
  return btn;
}

export function renderHome(root: HTMLElement, deps: HomeDeps): void {
  clear(root);
  const { stats } = deps;

  const title = el('h1', { class: 'brand' }, [
    el('span', { class: 'brand-word' }, [
      'Fin',
      el('span', { class: 'brand-hyphen', text: '-' }),
      'tastic',
    ]),
  ]);

  const tagline = el('p', { class: 'tagline' }, [
    'Seven prompts. Twenty-five seconds each. ',
    el('strong', { text: 'One dive.' }),
    ' The obvious answer barely moves you.',
  ]);

  const modes = el('div', { class: 'mode-grid' }, [
    modeCard(
      'daily',
      "Today's Dive",
      'The same seven prompts for everyone, today.',
      'Same prompts all day · replay freely',
      () => deps.onStart('daily'),
      true,
    ),
    modeCard(
      'dive',
      'Free Dive',
      'Seven fresh prompts, dealt the moment you ask.',
      'Play it again and again · new prompts every time',
      () => deps.onStart('dive'),
    ),
    modeCard(
      'endless',
      'Endless',
      'One prompt at a time, three tanks of air.',
      'A timeout costs a tank · a Fin-tastic answer gives one back',
      () => deps.onStart('endless'),
    ),
  ]);

  const resumeBanner = deps.hasSavedRun
    ? el('div', { class: 'resume-banner' }, [
        el('span', { text: 'A dive is still waiting for you.' }),
        (() => {
          const btn = el('button', { class: 'btn btn-primary btn-small' }, ['Resume']);
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            sfx.click();
            deps.onResume();
          });
          return btn;
        })(),
      ])
    : null;

  const statCards = el('div', { class: 'stat-grid' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'stat-card' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-value', text: formatDepth(stats.bestDepth) }),
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-label', text: 'Deepest dive' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'stat-card' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-value', text: formatPoints(stats.bestScore) }),
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-label', text: 'Best score' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'stat-card' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-value', text: String(stats.dives) }),
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-label', text: 'Dives' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'stat-card' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-value', text: formatDepth(stats.bestEndlessDepth) }),
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-label', text: 'Deepest endless' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'stat-card' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-value accent', text: String(stats.tierCounts.fintastic) }),
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-label', text: 'Fin-tastic' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'stat-card' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-value', text: String(stats.dailyStreak) }),
      el('span', { class: 'stat-card-label', text: 'Day streak' }),
    ]),
  ]);

  const howTo = el('details', { class: 'howto', open: true }, [
    el('summary', { text: 'How the scoring works' }),
    el('div', { class: 'howto-body' }, [

      }),
      el('p', {
        class: 'howto-note',
        text: `${PROMPTS_SANITISED.length} prompts and ${TOTAL_ANSWERS} accepted answers in the bank right now.`,

  root.append(
    el('div', { class: 'home' }, [

      resumeBanner,
      modes,
      statCards,
      howTo,
      el('p', { class: 'fine-print', text: 'Built as a love letter to rarest-answer quizzes. No account, no tracking, no daily cap.' }),
