}

===== src/ui/summary.ts =====
import type { PlayerStats, RunState } from '../game/types';
import { TIERS, TIER_ORDER, formatDepth, formatPoints, METRES_PER_POINT } from '../game/tiers';
import { zoneForDepth } from '../game/zones';
import { el, clear } from './dom';
import { sfx } from './audio';

export interface SummaryDeps {
  state: RunState;
  stats: PlayerStats;
  isBest: boolean;
  onAgain: (mode: RunState['mode']) => void;
  onHome: () => void;
}

export function renderSummary(root: HTMLElement, deps: SummaryDeps): void {
  const { state } = deps;
  clear(root);

  const zone = zoneForDepth(state.depth);
  const ending = state.ending?.kind ?? 'complete';
  const counts = state.results.reduce<Record<string, number>>((acc, result) => {
    if (result.tier) acc[result.tier] = (acc[result.tier] ?? 0) + 1;
    return acc;
  }, {});

  const accepted = state.results.filter((r) => r.tier !== null);
  const best = accepted.reduce((top, result) => (result.points > top ? result.points : top), 0);
  const average = accepted.length
    ? Math.round(accepted.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.points, 0) / accepted.length)
    : 0;

  const headlines: Record<string, { title: string; blurb: string; icon: string }> = {
    complete: {
      title: 'Dive complete',
      blurb: 'Seven prompts, one dive. Seven perfect answers would put you 7,000 m down, in the hadal trenches.',
      icon: '◆',
    },
    'out-of-air': {
      title: 'Out of air',
      blurb: 'Your last tank is gone. The dark keeps the rest.',
      icon: '○',
    },
    aborted: {
      title: 'Surfaced',
      blurb: 'You came back up early. The dive still counts.',
      icon: '↥',
    },
  };
  const head = headlines[ending] ?? headlines.complete!;

  const headline = el('div', { class: 'summary-head' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'summary-icon', text: head.icon }),
    el('div', {}, [
      el('h2', { class: 'summary-title', text: head.title }),
      el('p', { class: 'summary-blurb', text: head.blurb }),
    ]),
    deps.isBest ? el('span', { class: 'badge-new', text: 'New personal best' }) : null,
  ]);

  const depthBlock = el('div', { class: 'depth-block' }, [
    el('span', { class: 'depth-value', text: formatDepth(state.depth) }),
    el('span', { class: 'depth-zone', text: zone.name }),
    el('span', { class: 'depth-blurb', text: zone.blurb }),
  ]);

  const scoreBlock = el('div', { class: 'score-block' }, [
    el('div', { class: 'score-item' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'score-value', text: formatPoints(state.score) }),
      el('span', { class: 'score-label', text: 'points' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'score-item' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'score-value', text: String(accepted.length) }),
      el('span', { class: 'score-label', text: 'accepted' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'score-item' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'score-value', text: String(average) }),
      el('span', { class: 'score-label', text: 'avg points' }),
    ]),
    el('div', { class: 'score-item' }, [
      el('span', { class: 'score-value', text: String(best) }),
      el('span', { class: 'score-label', text: 'best answer' }),
    ]),
  ]);

  // Tier breakdown bar
  const breakdown = el('div', { class: 'breakdown' });
  const total = Math.max(1, accepted.length);
  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    const count = counts[tier] ?? 0;
    if (count === 0) continue;
    const meta = TIERS[tier];
    breakdown.append(
      el('div', { class: 'breakdown-row' }, [
        el('span', { class: 'breakdown-label', style: `color:${meta.color}` }, [
          el('span', { class: 'breakdown-emoji', text: meta.emblem }),
          meta.name,
        ]),
        el('span', { class: 'breakdown-bar' }, [
          el('span', {
            class: 'breakdown-fill',
            style: `width:${(count / total) * 100}%;background:${meta.color}`,
          }),
        ]),
        el('span', { class: 'breakdown-count', text: `×${count}` }),
      ]),
    );
  }
  if (accepted.length === 0) {
    breakdown.append(el('p', { class: 'breakdown-empty', text: 'Nothing accepted this dive.' }));
  }

  // Answer log — every attempt the player made, refusals included.
  const log = el('div', { class: 'answer-log' });
  const attempts = state.attempts?.length ? state.attempts : state.results;
  attempts.forEach((result, index) => {
    const meta = result.tier ? TIERS[result.tier] : null;
    const timedOut = result.outcome === 'timeout';
    const tierText = meta
      ? `${meta.name} +${result.points}${result.repeat ? ' · repeat' : ''}`
      : timedOut
        ? 'time up'
        : 'refused';
    log.append(
      el('div', {
        class: `log-row${result.tier ? '' : ' log-row-refused'}${result.repeat ? ' log-row-repeat' : ''}`,
      }, [
        el('span', { class: 'log-index', text: String(index + 1) }),
        el('span', { class: 'log-prompt', text: result.prompt.text }),
        el('span', { class: 'log-answer', text: result.answer ?? (timedOut ? '—' : result.raw) }),
        el('span', {
          class: 'log-tier',
          text: tierText,
          style: meta ? `color:${meta.color}` : '',
        }),
      ]),
    );
  });

  const againBtn = el('button', { class: 'btn btn-primary btn-wide' }, [
    state.mode === 'endless' ? 'Dive again ▸' : 'Another dive ▸',
  ]);
  againBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    sfx.click();
    deps.onAgain(state.mode);
  });

  const homeBtn = el('button', { class: 'btn btn-ghost btn-wide' }, ['Surface']);
  homeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    sfx.click();
    deps.onHome();
  });

  root.append(
    el('div', { class: 'summary' }, [
      headline,
      depthBlock,
      scoreBlock,
      breakdown,
      el('details', { class: 'log-details' }, [el('summary', { text: 'Every answer, in order' }), log]),
      el('div', { class: 'summary-actions' }, [againBtn, homeBtn]),
      el('p', {
        class: 'fine-print',
        text: `Each point is ${METRES_PER_POINT} metres. Best dive so far: ${formatDepth(deps.stats.bestDepth)}.`,
      }),
    ]),
  );
}

===== tests/engine.test.ts =====
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PROMPTS_SANITISED } from '../src/data/promptBank.ts';
import { GameEngine, type EngineEvent } from '../src/game/engine.ts';
import type { SavedAttempt, SavedRun } from '../src/game/types.ts';

test('a save taken after the last dive answer resumes as a finished run', () => {
  const prompts = PROMPTS_SANITISED.slice(0, 7);
  const results: SavedAttempt[] = prompts.map((prompt) => ({
    promptId: prompt.id,
    raw: prompt.answers.plankton[0] ?? '',
    answer: prompt.answers.plankton[0] ?? '',
    tier: 'plankton',
    points: 4,
    outcome: 'accepted',
    secondsUsed: 4,
    hinted: false,
    repeat: false,
  }));
  const saved: SavedRun = {
    mode: 'dive',
    seed: 1234,
    index: 6,
    score: 28,
    air: 3,
    results,
    promptIds: prompts.map((prompt) => prompt.id),
    savedAt: Date.now(),
  };

  const engine = new GameEngine();
  const events: EngineEvent[] = [];
  engine.on((event) => events.push(event));
  engine.restore(saved);

  assert.equal(engine.current.finished, true);
  assert.deepEqual(engine.current.ending, { kind: 'complete' });
  assert.equal(engine.current.index, 7);
  assert.equal(engine.current.results.length, 7);
  assert.equal(
    events.some((event) => event.type === 'prompt-start'),
    false,
  );
  assert.equal(events.at(-1)?.type, 'run-end');
});

test('restoring a save resumes at the exact next prompt in its queue', () => {
  const prompts = PROMPTS_SANITISED.slice(0, 7);
  const saved: SavedRun = {
    mode: 'dive',
    seed: 99,
    index: 3,
    score: 0,
    air: 3,
    results: [],
    promptIds: prompts.map((prompt) => prompt.id),
    savedAt: Date.now(),
  };

  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'window', {
    configurable: true,
    value: {
      setInterval: () => 1,
      clearInterval: () => undefined,
    },
  });

  try {
    const engine = new GameEngine();
    engine.restore(saved);

    assert.equal(engine.currentPrompt?.id, prompts[3]!.id);
    assert.equal(engine.current.index, 3);
    assert.deepEqual(engine.queueIds, prompts.map((prompt) => prompt.id));
  } finally {
    Reflect.deleteProperty(globalThis, 'window');
  }
});

===== tests/helpers.ts =====
import type { Prompt, TierId } from '../src/game/types.ts';
import { TIER_ORDER } from '../src/game/tiers.ts';

interface PromptOptions {
  id?: string;
  category?: string;
  aliases?: Record<string, string>;
}

/** Builds a tiny prompt fixture with empty buckets for anything not supplied. */
export function makePrompt(
  buckets: Partial<Record<TierId, string[]>>,
  options: PromptOptions = {},
): Prompt {
  const answers = {} as Record<TierId, string[]>;
  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) answers[tier] = [...(buckets[tier] ?? [])];

  return {
    id: options.id ?? 'fixture',
    text: 'A test prompt',
    category: options.category ?? 'Test',
    hint: 'A test hint',
    answers,
    aliases: options.aliases ?? {},
  };
}

export function tierAnswer(prompt: Prompt, tier: TierId): string {
  const answer = prompt.answers[tier][0];
  if (!answer) throw new Error(`Fixture has no answer in tier "${tier}"`);
  return answer;
}

===== tests/prompt-bank.test.ts =====
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  allAnswers,
  PROMPTS_SANITISED,
  PROMPT_BY_ID,
  TOTAL_ANSWERS,
} from '../src/data/promptBank.ts';
import { TIER_ORDER } from '../src/game/tiers.ts';
import { candidatesFor, gradeAnswer, normalise } from '../src/game/scoring.ts';

test('the prompt bank is large, varied and uniquely identified', () => {
  assert.ok(PROMPTS_SANITISED.length >= 100);
  assert.ok(TOTAL_ANSWERS >= 6000);

  const ids = new Set(PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => prompt.id));
  const texts = new Set(PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => prompt.text));
  assert.equal(ids.size, PROMPTS_SANITISED.length);
  assert.equal(texts.size, PROMPTS_SANITISED.length);
  assert.equal(PROMPT_BY_ID.size, PROMPTS_SANITISED.length);

  const categories = new Set(PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => prompt.category));
  assert.ok(categories.size >= 15);
});

test('every prompt is populated and every answer is clean data', () => {
  for (const prompt of PROMPTS_SANITISED) {
    assert.ok(prompt.id.trim().length > 0, prompt.id);
    assert.ok(prompt.text.trim().length > 0, prompt.id);
    assert.ok(prompt.category.trim().length > 0, prompt.id);
    assert.ok(prompt.hint.trim().length > 0, prompt.id);

    const byTier = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
    let total = 0;

    for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
      const bucket = prompt.answers[tier];
      assert.ok(bucket.length >= 5, `${prompt.id}: ${tier} has too few answers`);
      total += bucket.length;

      const seen = new Set<string>();
      for (const answer of bucket) {
        assert.equal(answer, answer.trim(), `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" has padding`);
        assert.equal(answer, answer.toLowerCase(), `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" is not lowercase`);
        assert.match(answer, /^[\x20-\x7e]+$/, `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" is not ASCII`);
        assert.ok(!answer.includes('?'), `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" contains "?"`);
        assert.ok(!seen.has(answer), `${prompt.id}: duplicate answer "${answer}" in ${tier}`);
        seen.add(answer);
      }
      byTier.set(tier, seen);
    }

    assert.ok(total >= 30, `${prompt.id} has only ${total} answers`);

    // An answer may not appear in two tiers, even with different punctuation.
    const normalised = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
      for (const answer of prompt.answers[tier]) {
        const key = normalise(answer);
        const previous = normalised.get(key);
        assert.equal(
          previous,
          undefined,
          `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" in ${tier} duplicates the ${previous ?? 'other'} tier`,
        );
        normalised.set(key, tier);
      }
    }
  }
});

test('aliases point at real answers and resolve cleanly', () => {
  for (const prompt of PROMPTS_SANITISED) {
    const answers = new Set(allAnswers(prompt));
    for (const [alias, target] of Object.entries(prompt.aliases ?? {})) {
      assert.equal(alias, alias.toLowerCase(), `${prompt.id}: alias "${alias}" is not lowercase`);
      assert.ok(answers.has(target), `${prompt.id}: alias "${alias}" targets missing "${target}"`);

      const result = gradeAnswer(prompt, alias);
      assert.equal(result.accepted, true, `${prompt.id}: alias "${alias}" was refused`);
      assert.equal(result.answer, target, `${prompt.id}: alias "${alias}" resolved to another answer`);
    }
  }
});

test('every accepted answer grades back to its own tier', () => {
  for (const prompt of PROMPTS_SANITISED) {
    for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
      for (const answer of prompt.answers[tier]) {
        const result = gradeAnswer(prompt, answer);
        assert.equal(result.accepted, true, `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" was refused`);
        assert.equal(result.tier, tier, `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" landed in the wrong tier`);
        assert.equal(
          normalise(result.answer ?? ''),
          normalise(answer),
          `${prompt.id}: "${answer}" resolved to another answer`,
        );
      }
    }
  }
});

test('answer keys never collide within a prompt', () => {
  for (const prompt of PROMPTS_SANITISED) {
    const owners = new Map<string, string>();

    for (const candidate of candidatesFor(prompt)) {
      const keys = [
        ...[...candidate.keys].map((key) => `key:${key}`),
        ...[...candidate.compactKeys].map((key) => `compact:${key}`),
      ];
      for (const key of keys) {
        const owner = owners.get(key);
        assert.equal(
          owner,
          undefined,
          `${prompt.id}: ${key} belongs to both "${owner ?? 'another answer'}" and "${candidate.answer}"`,
        );
        owners.set(key, candidate.answer);
      }
    }
  }
});

===== tests/scoring.test.ts =====
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  boundedLevenshtein,
  fuzzyDistanceLimit,
  gradeAnswer,
  hintAnswer,
  HINT_TIER,
  normalise,
  phraseVariants,
  wordVariants,
} from '../src/game/scoring.ts';
import { makePrompt } from './helpers.ts';

test('normalise folds case, accents, apostrophes and punctuation', () => {
  assert.equal(normalise('  Café déjà-vu!  '), 'cafe deja vu');
  assert.equal(normalise("O'Keeffe"), 'okeeffe');
  assert.equal(normalise('THE   Beatles!!!'), 'the beatles');
  assert.equal(normalise('C#'), 'c sharp');
  assert.equal(normalise('C++'), 'c plus plus');
});

test('word variants handle plurals without collapsing whole phrases', () => {
  assert.ok(wordVariants('tomato').has('tomatoes'));
  assert.ok(wordVariants('wolf').has('wolves'));
  assert.ok(wordVariants('hero').has('heroes'));

  const variants = phraseVariants('great white shark');
  assert.ok(variants.has('great white sharks'));
  assert.ok(!variants.has('shark'));
});

test('exact answers grade into the tier that owns them', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({ plankton: ['red'], rare: ['crimson'] });

  const common = gradeAnswer(prompt, '  RED! ');
  assert.deepEqual(
    { accepted: common.accepted, tier: common.tier, answer: common.answer },
    { accepted: true, tier: 'plankton', answer: 'red' },
  );

  const obscure = gradeAnswer(prompt, 'Crimson');
  assert.equal(obscure.tier, 'rare');
  assert.equal(obscure.answer, 'crimson');
});

test('aliases resolve to their canonical answer', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt(
    { plankton: ['red'], rare: ['crimson'] },
    { aliases: { scarlet: 'crimson' } },
  );

  const result = gradeAnswer(prompt, 'Scarlet!');
  assert.equal(result.accepted, true);
  assert.equal(result.tier, 'rare');
  assert.equal(result.answer, 'crimson');
  assert.equal(result.corrected, false);
});

test('short aliases do not pluralise into each other', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt(
    { plankton: ['pi'], schooler: ['psi'] },
    { aliases: { p: 'pi', ps: 'psi' } },
  );

  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'p').answer, 'pi');
  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'ps').answer, 'psi');
});

test('leading articles and plural forms are accepted', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({
    clever: ['the beatles'],
    schooler: ['tomato'],
  });

  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'Beatles').answer, 'the beatles');
  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'THE BEATLES').tier, 'clever');
  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'tomatoes').tier, 'schooler');
  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'tomato').tier, 'schooler');
});

test('missing spaces are accepted as an exact compact match', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({ rare: ['great white shark'] });

  const result = gradeAnswer(prompt, 'greatwhiteshark');
  assert.equal(result.accepted, true);
  assert.equal(result.answer, 'great white shark');
  assert.equal(result.corrected, false);
});

test('a unique one-edit typo is corrected, but not a short answer', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({
    plankton: ['cat'],
    deepcut: ['chameleon'],
  });

  const typo = gradeAnswer(prompt, 'chameleonn');
  assert.equal(typo.accepted, true);
  assert.equal(typo.tier, 'deepcut');
  assert.equal(typo.answer, 'chameleon');
  assert.equal(typo.corrected, true);

  assert.equal(gradeAnswer(prompt, 'cta').accepted, false);
});

test('fuzzy corrections are refused when two answers are equally close', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({ rare: ['lizard', 'wizard'] });

  const result = gradeAnswer(prompt, 'izard');
  assert.equal(result.accepted, false);
  assert.equal(result.tier, null);
  assert.equal(result.answer, null);
});

test('repeats are flagged without changing the grade', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({ plankton: ['red'] });
  const result = gradeAnswer(prompt, 'Red', new Set([normalise('red')]));

  assert.equal(result.accepted, true);
  assert.equal(result.tier, 'plankton');
  assert.equal(result.repeat, true);
});

test('fuzzy distance limits are conservative and bounded', () => {
  assert.equal(fuzzyDistanceLimit(3), 0);
  assert.equal(fuzzyDistanceLimit(4), 1);
  assert.equal(fuzzyDistanceLimit(12), 1);
  assert.equal(fuzzyDistanceLimit(13), 2);
  assert.equal(fuzzyDistanceLimit(21), 3);

  assert.equal(boundedLevenshtein('kitten', 'sitting', 3), 3);
  assert.equal(boundedLevenshtein('kitten', 'sitting', 2), 3);
});

test('hints reveal a rare example and cap the prompt', () => {
  const prompt = makePrompt({ rare: ['crimson'] });
  const hint = hintAnswer(prompt);

  assert.equal(hint.cappedTier, HINT_TIER);
  assert.match(hint.text, /crimson/);

  const empty = hintAnswer(makePrompt({ plankton: ['red'] }));
  assert.equal(empty.cappedTier, null);
});

===== tests/selection.test.ts =====
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PROMPTS_SANITISED } from '../src/data/promptBank.ts';
import { dailySeed, mulberry32 } from '../src/game/rng.ts';
import { selectDiveQueue, selectEndlessPrompt } from '../src/game/selection.ts';
import { makePrompt } from './helpers.ts';

test('dive queues are deterministic, unique and category-balanced', () => {
  const first = selectDiveQueue(PROMPTS_SANITISED, 123456, 7);
  const second = selectDiveQueue(PROMPTS_SANITISED, 123456, 7);

  assert.deepEqual(
    first.map((prompt) => prompt.id),
    second.map((prompt) => prompt.id),
  );
  assert.equal(first.length, 7);
  assert.equal(new Set(first.map((prompt) => prompt.id)).size, 7);
  assert.equal(new Set(first.map((prompt) => prompt.category)).size, 7);

  for (let i = 1; i < first.length; i++) {
    assert.notEqual(first[i]!.category, first[i - 1]!.category);
  }
});

test('dive selection avoids immediate repeats', () => {
  const prompts = ['a', 'b', 'c'].map((id) =>
    makePrompt({ plankton: [id] }, { id }),
  );
  const queue = selectDiveQueue(prompts, 77, 3);

  assert.equal(queue.length, 3);
  for (let i = 1; i < queue.length; i++) {
    assert.notEqual(queue[i]!.id, queue[i - 1]!.id);
  }
});

test('a small two-category bank is split evenly', () => {
  const prompts = [
    ...['a1', 'a2', 'a3'].map((id) =>
      makePrompt({ plankton: [id] }, { id, category: 'Alpha' }),
    ),
    ...['b1', 'b2', 'b3'].map((id) =>
      makePrompt({ plankton: [id] }, { id, category: 'Beta' }),
    ),
  ];
  const queue = selectDiveQueue(prompts, 9, 4);
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const prompt of queue) {
    counts.set(prompt.category, (counts.get(prompt.category) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  assert.equal(counts.get('Alpha'), 2);
  assert.equal(counts.get('Beta'), 2);
});

test('endless selection keeps going after the bank is exhausted', () => {
  const prompts = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, index) =>
    makePrompt(
      { plankton: [`answer-${index}`] },
      { id: `endless-${index}`, category: `Category ${index % 3}` },
    ),
  );
  const rng = mulberry32(4242);
  const used = new Set<string>();
  let lastPromptId: string | null = null;
  let lastCategory: string | null = null;

  for (let step = 0; step < 40; step++) {
    const chosen = selectEndlessPrompt({
      prompts,
      rng,
      usedPromptIds: used,
      lastPromptId,
      lastCategory,
      minDifficulty: 2,
      difficultyOf: () => 3,
    });

    assert.ok(prompts.includes(chosen));
    if (lastPromptId !== null && used.size < prompts.length) {
      assert.notEqual(chosen.id, lastPromptId);
    }

    used.add(chosen.id);
    lastPromptId = chosen.id;
    lastCategory = chosen.category;
  }

  assert.equal(used.size, prompts.length);
});

test('daily seeds depend on the date, not the clock time', () => {
  const morning = dailySeed(new Date(2026, 9, 22, 6, 30));
  const evening = dailySeed(new Date(2026, 9, 22, 21, 45));
  const nextDay = dailySeed(new Date(2026, 9, 23, 6, 30));

  assert.equal(morning, evening);
  assert.notEqual(morning, nextDay);
});

===== tests/storage.test.ts =====
import { beforeEach, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import type { RunState } from '../src/game/types.ts';
import { clearRun, loadRun, saveRun } from '../src/game/storage.ts';

const memory = new Map<string, string>();
const fakeStorage: Storage = {
  get length() {
    return memory.size;
  },
  clear() {
    memory.clear();
  },
  getItem(key) {
    return memory.get(key) ?? null;
  },
  key(index) {
    return [...memory.keys()][index] ?? null;
  },
  removeItem(key) {
    memory.delete(key);
  },
  setItem(key, value) {
    memory.set(key, String(value));
  },
};

Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', {
  configurable: true,
  value: fakeStorage,
});

beforeEach(() => {
  memory.clear();
});

function runState(): RunState {
  return {
    mode: 'dive',
    runId: 1,
    seed: 42,
    index: 2,
    results: [],
    attempts: [],
    score: 4,
    depth: 40,
    air: 3,
    finished: false,
  };
}

test('new saves preserve the exact prompt queue', () => {
  saveRun(runState(), ['bird', 'fruit', 'metal']);

  const loaded = loadRun();
  assert.ok(loaded);
  assert.equal(loaded.mode, 'dive');
  assert.equal(loaded.index, 2);
  assert.equal(loaded.score, 4);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.promptIds, ['bird', 'fruit', 'metal']);
});

test('legacy saves with embedded prompts and refused flags still load', () => {
  memory.set(
    'fin-tastic.run.v1',
    JSON.stringify({
      mode: 'endless',
      seed: 7,
      index: 1,
      score: 4,
      air: 2,
      savedAt: Date.now(),
      results: [
        {
          prompt: { id: 'bird' },
          raw: 'eagle',
          answer: 'eagle',
          tier: 'plankton',
          points: 4,
          secondsUsed: 3.2,
          hinted: false,
        },
      ],
      attempts: [
        {
          promptId: 'bird',
          raw: 'nope',
          refused: true,
          secondsUsed: 1.5,
        },
      ],
    }),
  );

  const loaded = loadRun();
  assert.ok(loaded);
  assert.equal(loaded.mode, 'endless');
  assert.equal(loaded.promptIds, undefined);
  assert.equal(loaded.results[0]?.promptId, 'bird');
  assert.equal(loaded.results[0]?.outcome, 'accepted');
  assert.equal(loaded.results[0]?.points, 4);
  assert.equal(loaded.attempts?.[0]?.outcome, 'refused');
  assert.equal(loaded.attempts?.[0]?.answer, null);
});

test('runs older than a week are discarded', () => {
  memory.set(
    'fin-tastic.run.v1',
    JSON.stringify({
      mode: 'dive',
      seed: 1,
      index: 0,
      score: 0,
      air: 3,
      results: [],
      attempts: [],
      savedAt: Date.now() - 8 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
    }),
  );

  assert.equal(loadRun(), null);
  assert.equal(memory.has('fin-tastic.run.v1'), false);
});

test('clearRun removes the active save', () => {
  saveRun(runState(), ['bird']);
  assert.ok(memory.has('fin-tastic.run.v1'));
  clearRun();
  assert.equal(loadRun(), null);
});

===== tests/suggestions.test.ts =====
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { MAX_SUGGESTIONS, MIN_AUTOCOMPLETE_LENGTH, suggestAnswers } from '../src/game/suggestions.ts';
import { makePrompt } from './helpers.ts';

const prompt = makePrompt(
  {
    plankton: [
      'rose',
      'rosebud',
      'redwood',
      'ruby',
      'raspberry',
      'rocket',
      'rainbow',
      'river',
      'great white shark',
    ],
    rare: ['crimson', 'chameleon'],
  },
  { aliases: { scarlet: 'crimson' } },
);

test('autocomplete waits for a meaningful query', () => {
  assert.equal(MIN_AUTOCOMPLETE_LENGTH, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(suggestAnswers(prompt, 'r'), []);
  assert.deepEqual(suggestAnswers(prompt, '  '), []);
});

test('suggestions are scoped to the current prompt and capped at five', () => {
  const suggestions = suggestAnswers(prompt, 're');
  assert.ok(suggestions.length > 0);
  assert.ok(suggestions.length <= 5);

  const accepted = new Set(Object.values(prompt.answers).flat());
  for (const suggestion of suggestions) {
    assert.ok(accepted.has(suggestion.answer));
  }

  const otherPrompt = makePrompt({ plankton: ['rhino'] });
  const other = suggestAnswers(otherPrompt, 'rh');
  assert.deepEqual(other.map((suggestion) => suggestion.answer), ['rhino']);
  assert.ok(!suggestions.some((suggestion) => suggestion.answer === 'rhino'));
});

test('aliases can surface a canonical answer without leaking its tier', () => {
  const suggestions = suggestAnswers(prompt, 'scar');
  const match = suggestions.find((suggestion) => suggestion.answer === 'crimson');

  assert.ok(match);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(match).sort(), [
    'answer',
    'fuzzy',
    'matchLength',
    'matchStart',
  ]);
  assert.equal('tier' in match, false);
  assert.equal('rank' in match, false);
});

test('exact matches rank ahead of prefixes', () => {
  const suggestions = suggestAnswers(prompt, 'rose');
  assert.equal(suggestions[0]?.answer, 'rose');
  assert.equal(suggestions[0]?.fuzzy, false);
});

test('word matches highlight the right span', () => {
  const suggestion = suggestAnswers(prompt, 'shark')[0];
  assert.equal(suggestion?.answer, 'great white shark');
  assert.equal(suggestion?.matchStart, 12);
  assert.equal(suggestion?.matchLength, 5);
});

test('compact queries highlight from the start', () => {
  const suggestion = suggestAnswers(prompt, 'greatwhite')[0];
  assert.equal(suggestion?.answer, 'great white shark');
  assert.equal(suggestion?.matchStart, 0);
  assert.equal(suggestion?.matchLength, 10);
});

test('a unique typo can be suggested as a fuzzy match', () => {
  const suggestion = suggestAnswers(prompt, 'chameleonn')[0];
  assert.equal(suggestion?.answer, 'chameleon');
  assert.equal(suggestion?.fuzzy, true);
});

test('the limit can be lowered but never exceeds the default cap', () => {
  assert.equal(MAX_SUGGESTIONS, 5);
  assert.equal(suggestAnswers(prompt, 'ro', 2).length, 2);
  assert.ok(suggestAnswers(prompt, 'ro', 50).length <= MAX_SUGGESTIONS);
});

===== src/data/promptBank.ts =====
import type { Prompt, TierId } from '../game/types';

/** Every tier id, in order. */
export const TIER_IDS: TierId[] = [
  'plankton',
  'clever',
  'schooler',
  'rare',
  'deepcut',
  'krillion',
];

/**
 * The prompt bank.
 *
 * Each prompt lists every accepted answer bucketed by how uncommon that
 * answer is. The buckets map 1:1 onto the six rarity tiers, so the tier a
 * player lands in is decided purely by which bucket their answer is in.
 *
 * Tier guide:
 *   plankton  - what the whole room says
 *   clever    - the "clever" second answer everyone thinks of next
 *   schooler  - a genuine, slightly better answer
 *   rare      - most players would never have said this
 *   deepcut   - specialist territory
 *   krillion  - somebody had to know this to say it
 *
 * Within a prompt the buckets are disjoint — an answer appears exactly once.
 */
function p(
  id: string,
  text: string,
  category: string,
  hint: string,
  tiers: [string[], string[], string[], string[], string[], string[]],
  aliases: Record<string, string> = {},
): Prompt {
  return {
    id,
    text,
    category,
    hint,
    answers: {
      plankton: tiers[0],
      clever: tiers[1],
      schooler: tiers[2],
      rare: tiers[3],
      deepcut: tiers[4],
      krillion: tiers[5],
    },
    aliases,
  };
}

export const PROMPTS: Prompt[] = [
  p(
    'bird',
    'Name a bird',
    'Nature',
    'A feathered animal with a beak, two legs and wings',

      ['sparrow', 'pigeon', 'eagle', 'crow', 'owl', 'robin', 'seagull', 'parrot', 'ostrich', 'penguin', 'chicken', 'duck', 'swan', 'starling'],

      ['osprey', 'kestrel', 'cockatoo', 'roadrunner', 'canary', 'condor', 'vulture', 'ibis', 'cormorant', 'pelican', 'nightingale', 'waxwing', 'bulbul', 'lorikeet'],
      ['kakapo', 'hoatzin', 'quetzal', 'shoebill', 'secretary bird', 'lyrebird', 'bird of paradise', 'hoopoe', 'bee-eater', 'turaco', 'hornbill', 'tropicbird', 'sparrowhawk', 'goshawk'],

  p(

      ['archlute', 'viola da gamba', 'violone', 'baryton', 'serpent', 'ophicleide', 'sarrusophone', 'saxhorn', 'musical saw', 'pedal steel guitar', 'bandoneon', 'harmonium', 'melodica', 'carillon'],
    ],
    {
      drums: 'drum',
      'guitar': 'guitar',
      'violin': 'violin',
    },
  ),
];



/**
 * Data hygiene pass, run once at module load.
 *
 * 1. De-duplicates answers so that an answer can only live in one tier. When
 *    an answer appears in several buckets the *commonest* bucket wins, because
 *    that is the honest description of the answer.
 * 2. Drops aliases whose target no longer exists.
 * 3. Normalises every answer to lowercase so lookups are predictable.
 */
function sanitise(prompt: Prompt): Prompt {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const answers = {} as Prompt['answers'];

  for (const tier of TIER_IDS) {
    const bucket: string[] = [];
    for (const answer of prompt.answers[tier] ?? []) {
      const key = answer.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!key || seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      bucket.push(key);
    }
    answers[tier] = bucket;
  }

  const aliases: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [alias, target] of Object.entries(prompt.aliases ?? {})) {
    const targetKey = target.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (seen.has(targetKey)) {
      aliases[alias.trim().toLowerCase()] = targetKey;
    }
  }

  return { ...prompt, answers, aliases };
}

export const PROMPTS_SANITISED: Prompt[] = PROMPTS.map(sanitise);

/**
 * Lookup by id. Built from the sanitised bank so a restored run always sees
 * the same answers the scorer will grade against.
 */
export const PROMPT_BY_ID = new Map(PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => [prompt.id, prompt]));

/** All answers for a prompt, flattened. */
export function allAnswers(prompt: Prompt): string[] {
  return Object.values(prompt.answers).flat();
}

export const TOTAL_ANSWERS = PROMPTS_SANITISED.reduce(
  (sum, prompt) => sum + allAnswers(prompt).length,
  0,
);

===== src/game/engine.ts =====
import type {
  GameMode,
  Prompt,
  PromptResult,
  RunEnding,
  RunState,
  SavedAttempt,
  SavedRun,
  TierId,
} from './types';
import { PROMPT_BY_ID, PROMPTS_SANITISED } from '../data/promptBank';
import { gradeAnswer, hintAnswer, normalise } from './scoring';
import {
  METRES_PER_POINT,
  PROMPTS_PER_DIVE,
  SECONDS_PER_PROMPT,
  STARTING_AIR,
  TIER_ORDER,
  tierIndex,
} from './tiers';
import { dailySeed, mulberry32, randomSeed, shuffle } from './rng';
import { zoneForDepth } from './zones';

export const MAX_AIR = 5;

/** Seconds burned per hint. */
export const HINT_SECOND_COST = 8;

export type EngineEvent =
  | { type: 'run-start'; state: RunState }
  | { type: 'prompt-start'; prompt: Prompt; index: number; total: number; remaining: number }
  | { type: 'tick'; remaining: number; fraction: number }
  | { type: 'graded'; result: PromptResult; accepted: boolean }
  | { type: 'hint'; text: string; cappedTier: TierId | null }
  | { type: 'timeout'; prompt: Prompt }
  | { type: 'state'; state: RunState }
  | { type: 'run-end'; state: RunState; ending: RunEnding };

type Listener = (event: EngineEvent) => void;

/**
 * Average tier weight of a prompt's answer list. Higher means the prompt
 * pushes players toward more obscure answers.
 */
function difficultyOf(prompt: Prompt): number {
  let total = 0;
  let count = 0;
  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    const weight = tierIndex(tier) + 1;
    for (const _ of prompt.answers[tier]) {
      total += weight;
      count += 1;
    }
  }
  return count === 0 ? 0 : total / count;
}

const DIFFICULTY = new Map<string, number>(
  PROMPTS_SANITISED.map((prompt) => [prompt.id, difficultyOf(prompt)]),
);

const BY_DIFFICULTY = PROMPTS_SANITISED.slice().sort(
  (a, b) => (DIFFICULTY.get(a.id) ?? 0) - (DIFFICULTY.get(b.id) ?? 0),
);

export class GameEngine {
  private listeners: Listener[] = [];
  private state: RunState;
  private rng: () => number;
  /** Prompt queue for the current run. */
  private queue: Prompt[] = [];
  /** Prompts already used in this run. */
  private usedPromptIds = new Set<string>();
  /** Normalised answers already given in this run. */
  private usedAnswers = new Set<string>();
  private timer: number | null = null;
  private deadline = 0;
  private startedAt = 0;
  /** Tier ceiling imposed by a spent hint. */
  private tierCap: TierId | null = null;
  /** Whether the current prompt has already been graded (accepted). */
  private locked = false;

  constructor() {
    const seed = randomSeed();
    this.rng = mulberry32(seed);
    this.state = {
      mode: 'dive',
      runId: 0,
      seed,
      index: 0,
      results: [],
      attempts: [],
      score: 0,
      depth: 0,
      air: STARTING_AIR,
      finished: false,
    };
  }

  on(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.push(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((l) => l !== listener);
    };
  }

  private emit(event: EngineEvent): void {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(event);
  }

  get current(): RunState {
    return this.state;
  }

  /** The prompt the player is looking at right now, if any. */
  get currentPrompt(): Prompt | null {
    return this.queue[this.state.index] ?? null;
  }

  // -- lifecycle ---------------------------------------------------------

  start(mode: GameMode): void {
    const seed = mode === 'daily' ? dailySeed() : randomSeed();
    this.rng = mulberry32(seed);
    this.usedPromptIds.clear();
    this.usedAnswers.clear();
    this.tierCap = null;

    this.state = {
      mode,
      runId: this.state.runId + 1,
      seed,
      index: 0,
      results: [],
      attempts: [],
      score: 0,
      depth: 0,
      air: STARTING_AIR,
      finished: false,
    };

    this.queue = mode === 'endless' ? [this.nextEndlessPrompt()] : this.buildDiveQueue(seed);
    this.emit({ type: 'run-start', state: this.state });
    this.beginPrompt();
  }

  /**
   * Rebuilds a saved run so the player can pick up where they left off.
   *
   * The saved run only carries prompt *ids*; the prompts themselves are
   * looked up in the bank again, so a restored run always grades against the
   * current data.
   *
   * The prompt order for a dive is derived purely from the seed, so it can be
   * reproduced exactly. The endless queue grows one prompt at a time and
   * cannot be reproduced, so it is rebuilt from the prompts already played
   * and a fresh one is dealt.
   */
  restore(snapshot: SavedRun): void {
    this.rng = mulberry32(snapshot.seed);
    this.usedPromptIds.clear();
    this.usedAnswers.clear();
    this.tierCap = null;
    this.locked = false;

    const results = snapshot.results
      .map((attempt) => this.resolveAttempt(attempt))
      .filter((result): result is PromptResult => result !== null);
    const attempts = (snapshot.attempts ?? snapshot.results)
      .map((attempt) => this.resolveAttempt(attempt))
      .filter((result): result is PromptResult => result !== null);

    // A run can be saved between answering a prompt and moving on, so the
    // saved index can point at an already-resolved prompt. Resume at the
    // first prompt that has not been resolved yet.
    const resumeIndex = Math.max(snapshot.index, results.length);

    this.state = {
      mode: snapshot.mode,
      runId: this.state.runId + 1,
      seed: snapshot.seed,
      index: resumeIndex,
      results,
      attempts,
      score: snapshot.score,
      depth: snapshot.score * METRES_PER_POINT,
      air: snapshot.air,
      finished: false,
    };

    for (const result of results) {
      this.usedPromptIds.add(result.prompt.id);
      if (result.answer) this.usedAnswers.add(normalise(result.answer));
    }

    if (snapshot.mode === 'endless') {
      // The endless queue cannot be reproduced from the seed, so the prompts
      // already played are replayed and a fresh one is dealt on top.
      this.queue = results.map((result) => result.prompt).slice(0, resumeIndex);
      while (this.queue.length < resumeIndex) {
        this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
      }
      this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
    } else {
      this.queue = this.buildDiveQueue(snapshot.seed);
      // Safety net: never point past the end of the queue.
      if (this.state.index >= this.queue.length) {
        this.state.index = this.queue.length - 1;
      }
    }

    this.emit({ type: 'run-start', state: this.state });
    this.beginPrompt();
  }

  /** Rebuilds a full result from a saved one, looking the prompt up by id. */
  private resolveAttempt(attempt: SavedAttempt): PromptResult | null {
    const prompt = PROMPT_BY_ID.get(attempt.promptId);
    if (!prompt) return null;
    return {
      prompt,
      raw: attempt.raw ?? '',
      answer: attempt.answer ?? null,
      tier: attempt.tier ?? null,
      points: attempt.points ?? 0,
      outcome: attempt.outcome ?? (attempt.tier ? 'accepted' : 'timeout'),
      secondsUsed: attempt.secondsUsed ?? 0,
      hinted: Boolean(attempt.hinted),
      repeat: Boolean(attempt.repeat),
    };
  }

  private buildDiveQueue(seed: number): Prompt[] {
    const rng = mulberry32(seed ^ 0x9e3779b9);
    return shuffle(PROMPTS_SANITISED, rng).slice(0, PROMPTS_PER_DIVE);
  }

  /**
   * Chooses the next endless prompt. Deeper water means harder prompts: we
   * require a minimum difficulty that grows with depth.
   */
  private nextEndlessPrompt(): Prompt {
    const depth = this.state.depth;
    const minDifficulty = 1.6 + Math.min(2.6, depth / 9000);
    const hard = BY_DIFFICULTY.filter(
      (prompt) => (DIFFICULTY.get(prompt.id) ?? 0) >= minDifficulty,
    );
    const pool = hard.length >= 6 ? hard : BY_DIFFICULTY.slice(-8);
    const fresh = pool.filter((prompt) => !this.usedPromptIds.has(prompt.id));
    const candidates = fresh.length > 0 ? fresh : pool;
    const chosen = candidates[Math.floor(this.rng() * candidates.length)]!;
    this.usedPromptIds.add(chosen.id);
    return chosen;
  }

  private beginPrompt(): void {
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt) return;
    this.locked = false;
    this.tierCap = null;
    this.startedAt = performance.now();
    this.deadline = this.startedAt + SECONDS_PER_PROMPT * 1000;
    this.emit({
      type: 'prompt-start',
      prompt,
      index: this.state.index,
      total: this.state.mode === 'endless' ? Infinity : PROMPTS_PER_DIVE,
      remaining: SECONDS_PER_PROMPT,
    });
    this.startTimer();
  }

  private startTimer(): void {
    this.stopTimer();
    this.timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const remaining = Math.max(0, (this.deadline - performance.now()) / 1000);
      this.emit({ type: 'tick', remaining, fraction: remaining / SECONDS_PER_PROMPT });
      if (remaining <= 0) {
        this.stopTimer();
        this.handleTimeout();
      }
    }, 100);
  }

  private stopTimer(): void {
    if (this.timer !== null) {
      window.clearInterval(this.timer);
      this.timer = null;
    }
  }

  /** Seconds already spent on the current prompt. */
  get secondsUsed(): number {
    return (performance.now() - this.startedAt) / 1000;
  }

  get remainingSeconds(): number {
    return Math.max(0, (this.deadline - performance.now()) / 1000);
  }

  // -- actions -----------------------------------------------------------

  /**
   * Submits an answer. A refused answer costs only the seconds it took and
   * leaves the prompt open; the player can try again with what's left.
   */
  submit(raw: string): PromptResult | null {
    if (this.state.finished || this.locked) return null;
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt) return null;

    const trimmed = raw.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return null;

    const graded = gradeAnswer(prompt, trimmed, this.usedAnswers);
    const secondsUsed = Math.min(SECONDS_PER_PROMPT, this.secondsUsed);

    if (!graded.accepted) {
      const refused: PromptResult = {
        prompt,
        raw: trimmed,
        answer: null,
        tier: null,
        points: 0,
        outcome: 'refused',
        secondsUsed,
        hinted: this.tierCap !== null,
        repeat: false,
      };
      // Refusals are logged, but the prompt stays open: the player can try
      // again with whatever is left of the clock.
      this.state.attempts.push(refused);
      this.emit({ type: 'graded', result: refused, accepted: false });
      // Save straight away so the refusal survives a refresh.
      this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
      return refused;
    }

    let tier = graded.tier!;
    if (this.tierCap !== null) {
      const capIndex = tierIndex(this.tierCap);
      const actualIndex = tierIndex(tier);
      if (actualIndex > capIndex) tier = this.tierCap;
    }

    const points = { plankton: 4, clever: 8, schooler: 16, rare: 32, deepcut: 64, krillion: 100 }[tier];

    const result: PromptResult = {
      prompt,
      raw: trimmed,
      answer: graded.answer,
      tier,
      points,
      outcome: 'accepted',
      secondsUsed,
      hinted: this.tierCap !== null,
      repeat: graded.repeat,
    };

    this.usedAnswers.add(normalise(graded.answer!));
    this.state.attempts.push(result);
    this.state.results.push(result);
    this.state.score += points;
    this.state.depth = this.state.score * METRES_PER_POINT;

    if (this.state.mode === 'endless') {
      if (tier === 'krillion') {
        this.state.air = Math.min(MAX_AIR, this.state.air + 1);
      }
    }

    this.locked = true;
    this.stopTimer();
    this.emit({ type: 'graded', result, accepted: true });
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
    return result;
  }

  /** Spends time (and a tier) to reveal a nudge. */
  useHint(): { text: string; cappedTier: TierId | null } | null {
    if (this.state.finished || this.locked) return null;
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt) return null;
    if (this.tierCap !== null) return null; // one hint per prompt

    const remaining = this.remainingSeconds;
    if (remaining <= HINT_SECOND_COST) return null;

    const hint = hintAnswer(prompt);
    this.tierCap = hint.cappedTier;
    this.deadline -= HINT_SECOND_COST * 1000;

    this.emit({ type: 'hint', text: hint.text, cappedTier: hint.cappedTier });
    return hint;
  }

  private handleTimeout(): void {
    const prompt = this.currentPrompt;
    if (!prompt || this.locked) return;

    const result: PromptResult = {
      prompt,
      raw: '',
      answer: null,
      tier: null,
      points: 0,
      outcome: 'timeout',
      secondsUsed: SECONDS_PER_PROMPT,
      hinted: this.tierCap !== null,
      repeat: false,
    };
    this.state.attempts.push(result);
    this.state.results.push(result);
    this.locked = true;
    this.emit({ type: 'timeout', prompt });
    this.emit({ type: 'graded', result, accepted: false });

    if (this.state.mode === 'endless') {
      this.state.air -= 1;
      if (this.state.air <= 0) {
        this.finish({ kind: 'out-of-air', fatalPrompt: prompt });
        return;
      }
    }

    // The prompt is over, but the player still gets to see the card and
    // decide when to carry on.
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
  }

  /** Moves to the next prompt. */
  advance(): void {
    if (this.state.finished) return;

    if (this.state.mode === 'endless') {
      this.state.index += 1;
      this.queue.push(this.nextEndlessPrompt());
      this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
      this.beginPrompt();
      return;
    }

    this.state.index += 1;
    if (this.state.index >= PROMPTS_PER_DIVE) {
      this.finish({ kind: 'complete' });
      return;
    }
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
    this.beginPrompt();
  }

  /** Ends the run early. */
  abort(): void {
    if (this.state.finished) return;
    this.stopTimer();
    this.finish({ kind: 'aborted' });
  }

  private finish(ending: RunEnding): void {
    this.stopTimer();
    this.state.finished = true;
    this.state.ending = ending;
    this.emit({ type: 'state', state: this.state });
    this.emit({ type: 'run-end', state: this.state, ending });
  }

  // -- reporting ---------------------------------------------------------

  get zone() {
    return zoneForDepth(this.state.depth);
  }

  get tierCounts(): Record<TierId, number> {
    const counts = {
      plankton: 0,
      clever: 0,
      schooler: 0,
      rare: 0,
      deepcut: 0,
      krillion: 0,
    } as Record<TierId, number>;
    for (const result of this.state.results) {
      if (result.tier) counts[result.tier] += 1;
    }
    return counts;
  }

  get acceptedCount(): number {
    return this.state.results.filter((r) => r.tier !== null).length;
  }

  get refusedCount(): number {
    return this.state.attempts.filter((r) => r.outcome === 'refused').length;
  }
}

export { PROMPTS_SANITISED };

===== src/game/rng.ts =====
/**
 * Small deterministic PRNG so that a "daily" dive is the same for everyone,
 * and so a run can be replayed exactly.
 */
export function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a |= 0;
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

export function randomSeed(): number {
  return Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

/** Seed for today's daily dive — same for every player on the same date. */
export function dailySeed(date = new Date()): number {
  const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth() + 1}-${date.getDate()}`;
  let hash = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < key.length; i++) {
    hash ^= key.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
  }
  return hash >>> 0;
}

/** Fisher–Yates using a seeded generator. */
export function shuffle<T>(items: readonly T[], rng: () => number): T[] {
  const out = items.slice();
  for (let i = out.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(rng() * (i + 1));
    const a = out[i]!;
    const b = out[j]!;
    out[i] = b;
    out[j] = a;
  }
  return out;
}

export function pick<T>(items: readonly T[], rng: () => number): T {
  return items[Math.floor(rng() * items.length)]!;
}

===== src/game/scoring.ts =====
import type { Prompt, TierId } from './types.ts';
import { TIERS, TIER_ORDER } from './tiers.ts';

/**
 * Normalises free text so that "The  Beatles", "beatles!" and "BEATLES" all
 * resolve to the same answer.
 */
export function normalise(input: string): string {
  return input
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') // strip accents
    .replace(/['\u2019]/g, '') // drop apostrophes: "birds' beaks" -> "birds beaks"
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

/** Irregular plurals worth special-casing. Both directions are listed. */
const IRREGULAR_PLURALS: Record<string, string> = {
  octopus: 'octopi',
  octopi: 'octopus',
  octopuses: 'octopi',
  cactus: 'cacti',
  cacti: 'cactus',
  cactuses: 'cacti',
  fungus: 'fungi',
  fungi: 'fungus',
  mouse: 'mice',
  mice: 'mouse',
  child: 'children',
  children: 'child',
  person: 'people',
  people: 'person',
  goose: 'geese',
  geese: 'goose',
  tooth: 'teeth',
  teeth: 'tooth',
  foot: 'feet',
  feet: 'foot',
  sheep: 'sheep',
  fish: 'fish',
  series: 'series',
  species: 'species',
  aircraft: 'aircraft',
  half: 'halves',
  knife: 'knives',
  leaf: 'leaves',
  loaf: 'loaves',
  shelf: 'shelves',
  wolf: 'wolves',
  dwarf: 'dwarves',
  thief: 'thieves',
  life: 'lives',
  wife: 'wives',
  hero: 'heroes',
  potato: 'potatoes',
  tomato: 'tomatoes',
  piano: 'pianos',
  radio: 'radios',
  photo: 'photos',
  zoo: 'zoos',
};

const LEADING_ARTICLES = new Set(['the', 'a', 'an']);

/** Words that never get pluralised or singularised on their own. */
const STOPWORDS = new Set([
  'the', 'a', 'an', 'of', 'and', 'or', 'in', 'on', 'at', 'to', 'for', 'from',
  'by', 'with', 'as', 'its', 'is', 'it', 'de', 'la', 'le', 'el',
]);

/**
 * Singular/plural variants of a single word.
 *
 * Only ever applied to one-word answers and to the individual words of a
 * longer phrase — never to a whole phrase, because "tiger shark" and
 * "hammerhead shark" must not collapse into each other.
 */
export function wordVariants(word: string): Set<string> {
  const out = new Set<string>([word]);

  const irregular = IRREGULAR_PLURALS[word];
  if (irregular) out.add(irregular);

  // plural -> singular
  if (word.length > 3 && word.endsWith('ies')) {
    out.add(`${word.slice(0, -3)}y`);
  }
  if (word.length > 4 && /(ches|shes|xes|zes|oes|ses)$/.test(word)) {
    out.add(word.slice(0, -2));
  } else if (word.length > 2 && word.endsWith('s') && !word.endsWith('ss') && !word.endsWith('us')) {
    out.add(word.slice(0, -1));
  }

  // singular -> plural
  if (!word.endsWith('s')) {
    out.add(`${word}s`);
    if (word.length > 2 && word.endsWith('y')) {
      out.add(`${word.slice(0, -1)}ies`);
    }
    if (/(ch|sh|x|z|o)$/.test(word)) {
      out.add(`${word}es`);
    }
    if (word.endsWith('f')) {
      out.add(`${word.slice(0, -1)}ves`);
    }
  }

  return out;
}

/**
 * Variants of a multi-word phrase. The whole phrase has to match: a player
 * who answers "shark" has not answered "tiger shark". Only light touches
 * are allowed — dropping a leading article and inflecting the content
 * words.
 */
export function phraseVariants(phrase: string): Set<string> {
  const words = phrase.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return new Set();
  if (words.length === 1) return wordVariants(words[0]!);

  const out = new Set<string>([words.join(' ')]);

  // Leading article: "the beatles" <-> "beatles".
  if (LEADING_ARTICLES.has(words[0]!)) {
    out.add(words.slice(1).join(' '));
  }

  // Inflect each content word in turn: "tiger sharks" <-> "tiger shark".
  words.forEach((word, index) => {
    if (word.length <= 3 || STOPWORDS.has(word)) return;
    for (const variant of wordVariants(word)) {
      if (variant === word) continue;
      const next = [...words];
      next[index] = variant;
      out.add(next.join(' '));
    }
  });

  return out;
}

/**
 * Produces a set of "keys" for a phrase.
 *
 * Single words get singular/plural variants, so "eagles" matches "eagle".
 * Multi-word phrases are matched whole, so "shark" does *not* match
 * "tiger shark" and the player has to earn the points they want.
 */
export function keysFor(phrase: string): Set<string> {
  const base = normalise(phrase);
  if (!base) return new Set();

  const words = base.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return new Set();
  if (words.length === 1) return wordVariants(words[0]!);
  return phraseVariants(base);
}

/** Does the player's answer match an accepted answer? */
function matches(inputKeys: Set<string>, acceptedKeys: Set<string>): boolean {
  for (const k of inputKeys) {
    if (acceptedKeys.has(k)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export interface GradeResult {
  accepted: boolean;
  tier: TierId | null;
  /** The canonical accepted answer text. */
  answer: string | null;
  /** True when the answer was already used in an earlier prompt this run. */
  repeat: boolean;
}

/**
 * Grades a player's answer against a prompt's accepted list.
 *
 * `usedAnswers` holds normalised answers already spent in this run so we can
 * flag repeats (and, optionally, refuse them).
 */
export function gradeAnswer(
  prompt: Prompt,
  input: string,
  usedAnswers: Set<string> = new Set(),
): GradeResult {
  const inputKeys = keysFor(input);
  if (inputKeys.size === 0) {
    return { accepted: false, tier: null, answer: null, repeat: false };
  }

  const aliasKeys = buildAliasKeys(prompt);

  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    for (const accepted of prompt.answers[tier]) {
      const acceptedKeys = keysFor(accepted);
      // Aliases can point a different spelling at this canonical answer.
      const extra = aliasKeys.get(accepted);
      if (extra) {
        for (const k of extra) acceptedKeys.add(k);
      }
      if (matches(inputKeys, acceptedKeys)) {
        const norm = normalise(accepted);
        return {
          accepted: true,
          tier,
          answer: accepted,
          repeat: usedAnswers.has(norm),
        };
      }
    }
  }

  return { accepted: false, tier: null, answer: null, repeat: false };
}

/**
 * Pre-computes the key sets for every alias of a prompt, grouped by the
 * canonical answer they point at.
 */
function buildAliasKeys(prompt: Prompt): Map<string, Set<string>> {
  const map = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  for (const [alias, target] of Object.entries(prompt.aliases ?? {})) {
    let keys = map.get(target);
    if (!keys) {
      keys = new Set<string>();
      map.set(target, keys);
    }
    for (const key of keysFor(alias)) keys.add(key);
  }
  return map;
}

/**
 * Picks a helpful nudge for a refused answer. We reveal the tier the player's
 * answer *sounds* like it belongs to without giving the answer away.
 */
export function refusalHint(prompt: Prompt, input: string): string {
  const hints: string[] = [];
  const text = input.toLowerCase();

  // Keyword nudges: if the player drifted into a neighbouring category.
  const keys = Object.keys(prompt.aliases ?? {});
  for (const alias of keys) {
    if (text.includes(alias.toLowerCase())) {
      hints.push(`"${alias}" is not an accepted answer for this prompt.`);
      break;
    }
  }

  hints.push(
    `Stay inside the category: ${prompt.hint}. The obvious answer is worth almost nothing here.`,
  );

  return hints.join(' ');
}

/**
 * The tier a hint reveals an example of — and the ceiling it puts on what
 * the prompt can be worth. A hint is a trade: you buy a sense of direction
 * with the top of the scoreboard.
 */
export const HINT_TIER: TierId = 'rare';

/**
 * Suggests a nearby-but-lower-tier answer for the hint system.
 */
export function hintAnswer(prompt: Prompt): {
  text: string;
  cappedTier: TierId | null;
} {
  const pool = prompt.answers[HINT_TIER] ?? [];
  const example = pool[0];
  if (!example) {
    return { text: 'No examples left to reveal — trust your instinct.', cappedTier: null };
  }
  const meta = TIERS[HINT_TIER];
  return {
    text: `A ${meta.name} answer looks like: “${example}”. Scoring is capped at ${meta.name} for this prompt.`,
    cappedTier: HINT_TIER,
  };
}

===== src/game/storage.ts =====
import type {
  AttemptOutcome,
  PlayerStats,
  PromptResult,
  RunState,
  SavedAttempt,
  SavedRun,
  TierId,
} from './types';
import { TIER_ORDER } from './tiers';

const STATS_KEY = 'krillion.stats.v1';
const RUN_KEY = 'krillion.run.v1';

export function emptyStats(): PlayerStats {
  return {
    dives: 0,
    endlessRuns: 0,
    totalPrompts: 0,
    bestDepth: 0,
    bestScore: 0,
    bestEndlessDepth: 0,
    tierCounts: {
      plankton: 0,
      clever: 0,
      schooler: 0,
      rare: 0,
      deepcut: 0,
      krillion: 0,
    },
    usedAnswers: [],
    dailyStreak: 0,
    lastDailyPlayed: null,
  };
}

function safeParse<T>(raw: string | null, fallback: T): T {
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

export function loadStats(): PlayerStats {
  const base = emptyStats();
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return base;
  const stored = safeParse<Partial<PlayerStats>>(localStorage.getItem(STATS_KEY), {});
  return {
    ...base,
    ...stored,
    tierCounts: { ...base.tierCounts, ...(stored.tierCounts ?? {}) },
    usedAnswers: stored.usedAnswers ?? [],
  };
}

export function saveStats(stats: PlayerStats): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STATS_KEY, JSON.stringify(stats));
  } catch {
    /* storage full or blocked — the game still works, it just forgets. */
  }
}

export function recordRun(stats: PlayerStats, run: {
  mode: 'dive' | 'endless';
  depth: number;
  score: number;
  tiers: Partial<Record<TierId, number>>;
  answered: string[];
}): PlayerStats {
  const next: PlayerStats = {
    ...stats,
    tierCounts: { ...stats.tierCounts },
    usedAnswers: [...stats.usedAnswers],
  };

  if (run.mode === 'dive') {
    next.dives += 1;
    next.bestDepth = Math.max(next.bestDepth, run.depth);
    next.bestScore = Math.max(next.bestScore, run.score);
  } else {
    next.endlessRuns += 1;
    next.bestEndlessDepth = Math.max(next.bestEndlessDepth, run.depth);
  }

  next.totalPrompts += run.answered.length;

  for (const tier of TIER_ORDER) {
    const count = run.tiers[tier] ?? 0;
    if (count) next.tierCounts[tier] = (next.tierCounts[tier] ?? 0) + count;
  }

  const known = new Set(next.usedAnswers);
  for (const answer of run.answered) known.add(answer);
  next.usedAnswers = [...known].slice(-600);

  return next;
}

export function recordDailyPlay(stats: PlayerStats, today: string): PlayerStats {
  if (stats.lastDailyPlayed === today) return stats;
  const yesterday = new Date();
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
  const yesterdayKey = `${yesterday.getFullYear()}-${yesterday.getMonth() + 1}-${yesterday.getDate()}`;
  const streak = stats.lastDailyPlayed === yesterdayKey ? stats.dailyStreak + 1 : 1;
  return { ...stats, dailyStreak: streak, lastDailyPlayed: today };
}

/**
 * Strips a result down to the bits worth keeping in localStorage. Only the
 * prompt *id* is stored — the prompt itself is looked up in the bank when the
 * run is restored, so a saved run stays tiny even after hundreds of answers.
 */
export function toSavedAttempt(result: PromptResult): SavedAttempt {
  return {
    promptId: result.prompt.id,
    raw: result.raw,
    answer: result.answer,
    tier: result.tier,
    points: result.points,
    outcome: result.outcome,
    secondsUsed: Math.round(result.secondsUsed * 10) / 10,
    hinted: result.hinted,
    repeat: result.repeat,
  };
}

export function saveRun(state: RunState): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  const run: SavedRun = {
    mode: state.mode,
    seed: state.seed,
    index: state.index,
    score: state.score,
    air: state.air,
    results: state.results.map(toSavedAttempt),
    attempts: state.attempts.map(toSavedAttempt),
    savedAt: Date.now(),
  };
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(RUN_KEY, JSON.stringify(run));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

const OUTCOMES: AttemptOutcome[] = ['accepted', 'refused', 'timeout'];

/**
 * Normalises one entry of a saved run. Handles older saves, which embedded
 * the whole prompt object and used a `refused` flag instead of an outcome.
 */
function normaliseAttempt(raw: unknown): SavedAttempt | null {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return null;
  const entry = raw as Record<string, unknown>;

  const embedded = entry.prompt as { id?: unknown } | undefined;
  const promptId =
    typeof entry.promptId === 'string'
      ? entry.promptId
      : typeof embedded?.id === 'string'
        ? embedded.id
        : null;
  if (!promptId) return null;

  const rawText = typeof entry.raw === 'string' ? entry.raw : '';
  const answer = typeof entry.answer === 'string' ? entry.answer : null;
  const tierId =
    typeof entry.tier === 'string' && TIER_ORDER.includes(entry.tier as TierId)
      ? (entry.tier as TierId)
      : null;

  let outcome: AttemptOutcome;
  if (OUTCOMES.includes(entry.outcome as AttemptOutcome)) {
    outcome = entry.outcome as AttemptOutcome;
  } else if (entry.refused === true) {
    outcome = 'refused';
  } else if (tierId) {
    outcome = 'accepted';
  } else if (rawText) {
    outcome = 'refused';
  } else {
    outcome = 'timeout';
  }

  return {
    promptId,
    raw: rawText,
    answer,
    tier: tierId,
    points: typeof entry.points === 'number' ? entry.points : 0,
    outcome,
    secondsUsed: typeof entry.secondsUsed === 'number' ? entry.secondsUsed : 0,
    hinted: entry.hinted === true,
    repeat: entry.repeat === true,
  };
}

export function loadRun(): SavedRun | null {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return null;
  const raw = safeParse<Record<string, unknown> | null>(localStorage.getItem(RUN_KEY), null);
  if (!raw) return null;

  // Runs older than a week are not worth resuming.
  if (typeof raw.savedAt !== 'number' || Date.now() - raw.savedAt > 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
    clearRun();
    return null;
  }

  const results = Array.isArray(raw.results)
    ? raw.results.map(normaliseAttempt).filter((a): a is SavedAttempt => a !== null)
    : [];
  const attempts = Array.isArray(raw.attempts)
    ? raw.attempts.map(normaliseAttempt).filter((a): a is SavedAttempt => a !== null)
    : results;

  return {
    mode: raw.mode === 'endless' || raw.mode === 'daily' ? raw.mode : 'dive',
    seed: typeof raw.seed === 'number' ? raw.seed : 1,
    index: typeof raw.index === 'number' ? raw.index : 0,
    score: typeof raw.score === 'number' ? raw.score : 0,
    air: typeof raw.air === 'number' ? raw.air : 3,
    results,
    attempts,
    savedAt: raw.savedAt,
  };
}

export function clearRun(): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  localStorage.removeItem(RUN_KEY);
}

===== src/game/tiers.ts =====
import type { TierId, TierMeta } from './types';

/**
 * The six rarity tiers. Points are what count; depth is points * 10 metres.
 */
export const TIERS: Record<TierId, TierMeta> = {
  plankton: {
    id: 'plankton',
    name: 'Plankton',
    points: 4,
    metres: 40,
    blurb: 'The answer on everyone’s lips. It barely moves you.',
    crowdShare: '~60% of players',
    color: '#7dd3fc',
    emblem: '🫧',
  },
  clever: {
    id: 'clever',
    name: 'Too Clever',
    points: 8,
    metres: 80,
    blurb: 'You thought of the second answer. So did everyone else.',
    crowdShare: '~20% of players',
    color: '#a5b4fc',
    emblem: '🫧',
  },
  schooler: {
    id: 'schooler',
    name: 'Schooler',
    points: 16,
    metres: 160,
    blurb: 'A real answer, and not a terrible one.',
    crowdShare: '~10% of players',
    color: '#c4b5fd',
    emblem: '🐟',
  },
  rare: {
    id: 'rare',
    name: 'Rare',
    points: 32,
    metres: 320,
    blurb: 'Most people would never have said that.',
    crowdShare: '~5% of players',
    color: '#f0abfc',
    emblem: '🐠',
  },
  deepcut: {
    id: 'deepcut',
    name: 'Deep Cut',
    points: 64,
    metres: 640,
    blurb: 'You went somewhere the crowd never went.',
    crowdShare: '~1% of players',
    color: '#fbbf24',
    emblem: '🦑',
  },
  krillion: {
    id: 'krillion',
    name: 'One in a Krillion',
    points: 100,
    metres: 1000,
    blurb: 'Nobody else reached this. The whole room is looking at you.',
    crowdShare: '1 in a million',
    color: '#f472b6',
    emblem: '🐜',
  },
};

export const TIER_ORDER: TierId[] = [
  'plankton',
  'clever',
  'schooler',
  'rare',
  'deepcut',
  'krillion',
];

export const METRES_PER_POINT = 10;

/** Perfect dive = 7 prompts x 100 points = 700 points = 7000 m. */
export const PROMPTS_PER_DIVE = 7;
export const POINTS_PER_PROMPT = 100;
export const PERFECT_DIVE_POINTS = PROMPTS_PER_DIVE * POINTS_PER_PROMPT;
export const PERFECT_DIVE_DEPTH = PERFECT_DIVE_POINTS * METRES_PER_POINT;

export const SECONDS_PER_PROMPT = 25;

/** Endless mode starts with three tanks of air. */
export const STARTING_AIR = 3;

export function tierIndex(id: TierId): number {
  return TIER_ORDER.indexOf(id);
}

export function pointsToDepth(points: number): number {
  return points * METRES_PER_POINT;
}

export function formatDepth(metres: number): string {
  if (metres >= 1000) {
    return `${(metres / 1000).toFixed(metres >= 10_000 ? 0 : 2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} km`;
  }
  return `${Math.round(metres).toLocaleString('en-US')} m`;
}

export function formatPoints(points: number): string {
  return points.toLocaleString('en-US');
}

===== src/game/types.ts =====
/**
 * Core type definitions for the Krillion Dive game.
 */

/** The six rarity tiers an accepted answer can land in, worst first. */
export type TierId =
  | 'plankton'
  | 'clever'
  | 'schooler'
  | 'rare'
  | 'deepcut'
  | 'krillion';

export interface TierMeta {
  id: TierId;
  name: string;
  points: number;
  /** Depth in metres gained per answer in this tier. */
  metres: number;
  /** Short flavour text shown on the result card. */
  blurb: string;
  /** Approximate share of the crowd that gives an answer in this tier. */
  crowdShare: string;
  /** Accent colour (CSS colour) used for the tier glow. */
  color: string;
  /** Emblem glyph. */
  emblem: string;
}

export interface Prompt {
  id: string;
  /** The prompt text shown to the player. */
  text: string;
  /** Broad category, shown as a chip before you use a hint. */
  category: string;
  /** Narrower category revealed by the hint. */
  hint: string;
  /** Accepted answers, bucketed by rarity tier. */
  answers: Record<TierId, string[]>;
  /**
   * Alternative spellings / abbreviations that should resolve to a canonical
   * answer. Keys are matched against the normalised player input.
   */
  aliases?: Record<string, string>;
}

/** How a single attempt at a prompt ended. */
export type AttemptOutcome = 'accepted' | 'refused' | 'timeout';

/** A single answered prompt inside a run. */
export interface PromptResult {
  prompt: Prompt;
  /** Exactly what the player typed. */
  raw: string;
  /** Canonical accepted answer, when the answer was accepted. */
  answer: string | null;
  tier: TierId | null;
  points: number;
  /** How the attempt ended. */
  outcome: AttemptOutcome;
  /** Seconds burned on this prompt, including refusals. */
  secondsUsed: number;
  /** Whether a hint was spent. */
  hinted: boolean;
  /** True when the player had already used this answer earlier in the run. */
  repeat: boolean;
}

export type GameMode = 'dive' | 'endless' | 'daily';

export interface RunState {
  mode: GameMode;
  /** Monotonic identifier for this run (dive number). */
  runId: number;
  /** Seed for the prompt order, so a run can be replayed exactly. */
  seed: number;
  index: number;
  /** One entry per resolved prompt, in prompt order (accepted or timed out). */
  results: PromptResult[];
  /** Every attempt the player made, refused answers included, in order. */
  attempts: PromptResult[];
  score: number;
  /** Depth in metres. */
  depth: number;
  /** Endless mode: remaining tanks of air. */
  air: number;
  /** True once the run is over. */
  finished: boolean;
  /** Set when the run ends, for the summary screen. */
  ending?: RunEnding;
}

export interface RunEnding {
  kind: 'complete' | 'out-of-air' | 'aborted';
  /** The prompt the player died on, if any. */
  fatalPrompt?: Prompt;
}

export interface PlayerStats {
  dives: number;
  endlessRuns: number;
  totalPrompts: number;
  bestDepth: number;
  bestScore: number;
  bestEndlessDepth: number;
  /** Count of each tier ever landed on. */
  tierCounts: Record<TierId, number>;
  /** Answers the player has already used, so we can flag repeats. */
  usedAnswers: string[];
  /** ISO date strings of days the daily was played. */
  dailyStreak: number;
  lastDailyPlayed: string | null;
}

/**
 * A lightweight record of an attempt. Only the prompt *id* is stored, so a
 * saved run stays small in localStorage even with hundreds of answers.
 */
export interface SavedAttempt {
  promptId: string;
  raw: string;
  answer: string | null;
  tier: TierId | null;
  points: number;
  outcome: AttemptOutcome;
  secondsUsed: number;
  hinted: boolean;
  repeat: boolean;
}

export interface SavedRun {
  mode: GameMode;
  seed: number;
  index: number;
  score: number;
  air: number;
  results: SavedAttempt[];
  /** Every attempt, refused answers included. */
  attempts?: SavedAttempt[];
  savedAt: number;
}

===== src/game/zones.ts =====
/**
 * The ocean the player sinks through.
 *
 * The first 10,935 m are real. Past Challenger Deep the map is invented and
 * hand-drawn, because a perfect dive only earns you to the trench floor and
 * endless mode deserves somewhere stranger to go.
 */

export interface Zone {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  /** Depth range in metres. */
  from: number;
  to: number;
  /** Short description shown on the depth gauge. */
  blurb: string;
  /** Surface colour of the zone, used for the backdrop gradient. */
  color: string;
  /** Deeper colour of the zone. */
  deep: string;
  /** True for the invented zones below Challenger Deep. */
  invented: boolean;
}

    color: '#010307',
    deep: '#000000',
    invented: true,
  },

    id: 'krillion',
    name: 'One in a Krillion',
    from: 250000,
    to: Infinity,
    blurb: 'Nobody has ever written this down. You are here.',
    color: '#000000',
    deep: '#000000',
    invented: true,
  },

export const CHALLENGER_DEEP = 10935;

/** The zone a given depth belongs to. */
export function zoneForDepth(depth: number): Zone {
  for (const zone of ZONES) {
    if (depth < zone.to) return zone;
  }
  return ZONES[ZONES.length - 1]!;
}

/** Progress through the current zone, 0..1. */
export function zoneProgress(depth: number): number {
  const zone = zoneForDepth(depth);
  if (!Number.isFinite(zone.to)) return 1;
  const span = zone.to - zone.from;
  if (span <= 0) return 1;
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (depth - zone.from) / span));
}

/** How far through the whole ocean column the player is, 0..1. */
export function totalProgress(depth: number): number {
  const max = ZONES[ZONES.length - 1]!.from;
  return Math.min(1, depth / max);
}

===== src/main.ts =====
import './styles/main.css';
import { App } from './ui/app';

const root = document.getElementById('screen');
const canvas = document.getElementById('ocean-canvas') as HTMLCanvasElement | null;

if (!root || !canvas) {
  throw new Error('Krillion: missing mount points');
}

const app = new App(root, canvas);

// Exposed so a run can be poked at from the browser console:
//   krillion.engine.current
declare global {
  interface Window {
    krillion: App;
  }
}
window.krillion = app;
